var array<float> FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS = array.from(1.000, 1.236, 1.382, 1.500, 1.618, 1.786, 2.000)
f_price(float value) =>
    na(value) ? "-" : str.tostring(value, format.mintick)
f_json_number(float value) =>
    na(value) ? "null" : str.tostring(value, format.mintick)

f_bot_payload(string source, string eventCode, float eventPrice, int direction, float entryPrice, float stopPrice, float target1Price, float target2Price, float target3Price, int targetCount, int score, int scoreMaximum, bool counterTrend) =>
    "{\"v\":2,\"s\":\"" + source + "\",\"e\":\"" + eventCode + "\",\"x\":\"" + syminfo.tickerid + "\",\"f\":\"" + timeframe.period + "\",\"p\":" + f_json_number(eventPrice) + ",\"d\":" + str.tostring(direction) + ",\"en\":" + f_json_number(entryPrice) + ",\"sl\":" + f_json_number(stopPrice) + ",\"t1\":" + f_json_number(target1Price) + ",\"t2\":" + f_json_number(target2Price) + ",\"t3\":" + f_json_number(target3Price) + ",\"n\":" + str.tostring(targetCount) + ",\"q\":" + str.tostring(score) + ",\"qm\":" + str.tostring(scoreMaximum) + ",\"ct\":" + str.tostring(counterTrend) + "}"

f_market_tick(float value) =>
    syminfo.prefix == "TADAWUL" and syminfo.type == "stock" ? value < 25 ? 0.01 : value < 50 ? 0.02 : value < 100 ? 0.05 : value < 250 ? 0.10 : value < 500 ? 0.20 : 0.50 : syminfo.mintick

f_floor_to_tick(float value) =>
    float first = na(value) ? na : math.floor(value / f_market_tick(value) + 1e-10) * f_market_tick(value)
    na(first) ? na : math.floor(value / f_market_tick(first) + 1e-10) * f_market_tick(first)

f_ceil_to_tick(float value) =>
    float first = na(value) ? na : math.ceil(value / f_market_tick(value) - 1e-10) * f_market_tick(value)
    na(first) ? na : math.ceil(value / f_market_tick(first) - 1e-10) * f_market_tick(first)

f_next_fib_extension_level(bool isLong, float anchorLevel, float swingDistance, float entryPrice, float requiredDistance, float maximumDistance) =>
    float selectedLevel = na
    for ratioIndex = 0 to array.size(FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS) - 1
        float extensionRatio = array.get(FIB_EXTENSION_RATIOS, ratioIndex)
        float extensionOffset = swingDistance * math.max(extensionRatio - 1.0, 0.0)
        float extensionLevel = isLong ? anchorLevel + extensionOffset : anchorLevel - extensionOffset
        float distanceFromEntry = isLong ? extensionLevel - entryPrice : entryPrice - extensionLevel
        bool levelValid = distanceFromEntry >= requiredDistance and distanceFromEntry <= maximumDistance
        if na(selectedLevel) and levelValid
            selectedLevel := extensionLevel
    selectedLevel

f_build_fib_extension_targets(bool isLong, float anchorLevel, float swingDistance, float entryPrice, float minimumDistance1, float minimumDistance2, float minimumDistance3, float maximumDistance1, float maximumDistance2, float maximumDistance3, float minimumGap) =>
    float selectedLevel1 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, minimumDistance1, maximumDistance1)
    float selectedDistance1 = na(selectedLevel1) ? minimumDistance1 : isLong ? selectedLevel1 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel1
    float boundedDistance1 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance1, minimumDistance1), maximumDistance1)
    float requiredDistance2 = math.max(minimumDistance2, boundedDistance1 + minimumGap)
    float selectedLevel2 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, requiredDistance2, maximumDistance2)
    float selectedDistance2 = na(selectedLevel2) ? requiredDistance2 : isLong ? selectedLevel2 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel2
    float boundedDistance2 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance2, requiredDistance2), maximumDistance2)
    float requiredDistance3 = math.max(minimumDistance3, boundedDistance2 + minimumGap)
    float selectedLevel3 = f_next_fib_extension_level(isLong, anchorLevel, swingDistance, entryPrice, requiredDistance3, maximumDistance3)
    float selectedDistance3 = na(selectedLevel3) ? requiredDistance3 : isLong ? selectedLevel3 - entryPrice : entryPrice - selectedLevel3
    float boundedDistance3 = math.min(math.max(selectedDistance3, requiredDistance3), maximumDistance3)
    float target1 = isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance1 : entryPrice - boundedDistance1
    float target2 = isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance2 : entryPrice - boundedDistance2
    float target3 = isLong ? entryPrice + boundedDistance3 : entryPrice - boundedDistance3
    [target1, target2, target3, selectedLevel1, selectedLevel2, selectedLevel3]


f_text_code(array<string> values, int code) =>
    array.get(values, math.max(0, math.min(code, array.size(values) - 1)))

f_zone_pattern_text(int patternCode, int baseCount) =>
    string patternName = patternCode == 1 ? "طلب DBR" : patternCode == 2 ? "طلب RBR" : patternCode == -1 ? "عرض RBD" : patternCode == -2 ? "عرض DBD" : "قاعدة غير مصنفة"
    patternName + (baseCount > 0 ? " | قاعدة " + str.tostring(baseCount) + " شمعة" : "")

f_zone_origin(bool demandSide, int lookbackBars, float maximumBaseBodyRatio) =>
    bool originReady = false
    int farthestOffset = na
    int baseCandleCount = 0
    float proximalLine = na
    float distalLine = na
    for offset = 1 to lookbackBars
        float originCandleRange = high[offset] - low[offset]
        bool candleReady = not na(originCandleRange) and originCandleRange > syminfo.mintick * 0.50
        float candleBodyRatio = candleReady ? math.abs(close[offset] - open[offset]) / originCandleRange : 1.0
        bool baseCandle = candleReady and candleBodyRatio <= maximumBaseBodyRatio
        bool oppositeCandle = candleReady and (demandSide ? close[offset] < open[offset] : close[offset] > open[offset])
        bool firstOriginCandle = not originReady and (baseCandle or oppositeCandle)
        bool adjacentOlderBase = originReady and offset == farthestOffset + 1 and baseCandle and baseCandleCount < 6
        if firstOriginCandle or adjacentOlderBase
            originReady := true
            farthestOffset := offset
            baseCandleCount += baseCandle ? 1 : 0
            float bodyHigh = math.max(open[offset], close[offset])
            float bodyLow = math.min(open[offset], close[offset])
            proximalLine := na(proximalLine) ? (demandSide ? bodyHigh : bodyLow) : demandSide ? math.max(proximalLine, bodyHigh) : math.min(proximalLine, bodyLow)
            distalLine := na(distalLine) ? (demandSide ? low[offset] : high[offset]) : demandSide ? math.min(distalLine, low[offset]) : math.max(distalLine, high[offset])
        else if originReady
            break
    int incomingOffset = not na(farthestOffset) ? farthestOffset + 1 : na
    bool incomingAvailable = not na(incomingOffset) and incomingOffset <= lookbackBars
    bool incomingDown = incomingAvailable and close[incomingOffset] < open[incomingOffset]
    bool incomingUp = incomingAvailable and close[incomingOffset] > open[incomingOffset]
    bool incomingDirectional = incomingDown or incomingUp
    int patternCode = not incomingDirectional ? 0 : demandSide ? (incomingDown ? 1 : 2) : (incomingUp ? -1 : -2)
    [originReady, farthestOffset, proximalLine, distalLine, baseCandleCount, patternCode]

f_zone_geometry_valid(float topLevel, float bottomLevel, float atrValue) =>
    float zoneWidth = topLevel - bottomLevel
    float zoneMidpoint = (topLevel + bottomLevel) * 0.50
    float atrWidthLimit = math.max(atrValue * (timeframe.isintraday ? 1.50 : 2.00), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float percentWidthLimit = math.max(zoneMidpoint * (timeframe.isintraday ? 0.04 : 0.08), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    not na(topLevel) and not na(bottomLevel) and not na(atrValue) and bottomLevel > 0 and zoneWidth >= syminfo.mintick and zoneWidth <= atrWidthLimit and zoneWidth <= percentWidthLimit

f_stop_atr_limits(float seconds) =>
    float minimumMultiple = seconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 1.10 : seconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 1.20 : seconds <= 31 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 1.30 : 1.40
    float maximumMultiple = seconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 5.00 : seconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 6.00 : seconds <= 31 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 7.00 : 8.00
    [minimumMultiple, maximumMultiple]

f_clear_line(line objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        line.delete(objectId)
    line(na)

f_clear_label(label objectId) =>
    if not na(objectId)
        label.delete(objectId)
    label(na)


f_zone_state_valid(float topLevel, float bottomLevel, float creationAtr, float creationClose) =>
    float mid = (topLevel + bottomLevel) * 0.50
    float atr0 = math.max(nz(creationAtr, 0.0), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float limit = math.max(atr0 * (timeframe.isintraday ? 4.0 : 6.0), nz(creationClose, mid) * (timeframe.isintraday ? 0.08 : 0.12))
    f_zone_geometry_valid(topLevel, bottomLevel, atr0) and not na(creationClose) and creationClose > 0 and math.abs(mid - creationClose) <= limit

f_new_plan_line(float level, color lineColor, int transparency, string lineStyle) =>
    line.new(bar_index, level, bar_index + 1, level, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(lineColor, transparency), style = lineStyle, width = 1, force_overlay = true)

f_replace_plan_line(line currentId, bool enabled, float level, color lineColor, int transparency, string lineStyle) =>
    f_clear_line(currentId)
    enabled ? f_new_plan_line(level, lineColor, transparency, lineStyle) : na

f_nearest_price_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float candidate1, float candidate2, float candidate3, float candidate4, float candidate5, float candidate6, float candidate7, float candidate8, float candidate9, float candidate10) =>
    array<float> candidateLevels = array.from(candidate1, candidate2, candidate3, candidate4, candidate5, candidate6, candidate7, candidate8, candidate9, candidate10)
    float nearestLevel = na
    for candidateIndex = 0 to array.size(candidateLevels) - 1
        float candidateLevel = array.get(candidateLevels, candidateIndex)
        bool candidateValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
        bool candidateNearer = candidateValid and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove and candidateLevel < nearestLevel) or (not searchAbove and candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
        if candidateNearer
            nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel

// المستوى التالي بعد الأقرب، مع دمج المستويات المتقاربة كي لا يرسم المؤشر
// خطين للمقاومة أو الدعم نفسيهما بسبب اختلاف بسيط بين Pivot وHTF وكلاستر.
f_next_price_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float firstLevel, float minimumSeparation, float candidate1, float candidate2, float candidate3, float candidate4, float candidate5, float candidate6, float candidate7, float candidate8, float candidate9, float candidate10) =>
    array<float> candidateLevels = array.from(candidate1, candidate2, candidate3, candidate4, candidate5, candidate6, candidate7, candidate8, candidate9, candidate10)
    float nextLevel = na
    for candidateIndex = 0 to array.size(candidateLevels) - 1
        float candidateLevel = array.get(candidateLevels, candidateIndex)
        bool sideValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
        bool distinct = sideValid and not na(firstLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= firstLevel + minimumSeparation : candidateLevel <= firstLevel - minimumSeparation)
        bool nearer = distinct and (na(nextLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nextLevel : candidateLevel > nextLevel))
        if nearer
            nextLevel := candidateLevel
    nextLevel


// أقرب قمة/قاع تاريخي مؤكد أمام الصفقة. تُستبعد المحاور القديمة جدًا
// والمستويات الواقعة خلف السعر أو خارج غلاف الهدف الحالي.
f_nearest_stored_pivot(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, int maximumAgeMilliseconds) =>
    float nearestLevel = na
    int count = math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), array.size(pivotTimes))
    if count > 0
        for index = 0 to count - 1
            float candidateLevel = array.get(prices, index)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, index)
            int candidateTime = array.get(pivotTimes, index)
            float candidateDistance = not na(candidateLevel) ? math.abs(candidateLevel - currentPrice) : na
            bool positionValid = not na(candidateBar) and candidateBar >= 0 and candidateBar <= bar_index
            bool ageValid = positionValid and not na(candidateTime) and time >= candidateTime and (maximumAgeMilliseconds <= 0 or time - candidateTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds)
            bool sideValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
            bool distanceValid = sideValid and (na(maximumDistance) or maximumDistance <= 0 or candidateDistance <= maximumDistance)
            if ageValid and distanceValid and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nearestLevel : candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
                nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel

// مستوى تاريخي صالح للكسر: محور قديم ومؤكد ولم يسبق إغلاق السعر خلفه.
// يمنع هذا اعتبار كل Pivot محلي أو إعادة كسر مستوى مستهلك حدثًا تاريخيًا جديدًا.
f_nearest_unbroken_historical_pivot(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, array<bool> brokenStates, int minimumAgeBars, int maximumAgeMilliseconds) =>
    float nearestLevel = na
    int count = math.min(math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), array.size(pivotTimes)), array.size(brokenStates))
    if count > 0
        for index = 0 to count - 1
            float candidateLevel = array.get(prices, index)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, index)
            int candidateTime = array.get(pivotTimes, index)
            bool candidateBroken = array.get(brokenStates, index)
            int candidateAgeBars = not na(candidateBar) ? bar_index - candidateBar : 0
            bool ageValid = not na(candidateBar) and candidateBar >= 0 and candidateBar <= bar_index and not na(candidateTime) and candidateAgeBars >= minimumAgeBars and time >= candidateTime and (maximumAgeMilliseconds <= 0 or time - candidateTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds)
            bool sideValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance)
            if ageValid and sideValid and not candidateBroken and (na(nearestLevel) or (searchAbove ? candidateLevel < nearestLevel : candidateLevel > nearestLevel))
                nearestLevel := candidateLevel
    nearestLevel


// ترتيب المصادر: 1) قمم/قيعان ودعم/مقاومة، 2) ترند/قناة،
// 3) فيبوناتشي، 4) متوسطات، 5) شموع طويلة أو عالية الحجم.
// اجتماع مصدرين أو أكثر يضيف علاوة كلاستر، لذلك قد يتفوق الكلاستر على مستوى منفرد.
f_ranked_cluster_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, float tolerance, array<float> levels) =>
    float bestLevel = na
    int bestScore = -1
    int bestCount = 0
    int bestRank = 99
    float bestDistance = 1e20
    int lastIndex = array.size(levels) - 1
    for candidateIndex = 0 to lastIndex
        float candidateLevel = array.get(levels, candidateIndex)
        float candidateDistance = not na(candidateLevel) ? math.abs(candidateLevel - currentPrice) : na
        bool candidateValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or candidateDistance <= maximumDistance)
        if candidateValid
            int clusterCount = 1
            int clusterScore = candidateIndex == 0 ? 24 : candidateIndex == 1 ? 16 : candidateIndex == 2 ? 12 : candidateIndex <= 5 ? 5 : candidateIndex <= 9 ? 3 : 1
            for otherIndex = 0 to lastIndex
                if otherIndex != candidateIndex
                    float otherLevel = array.get(levels, otherIndex)
                    float otherDistance = not na(otherLevel) ? math.abs(otherLevel - currentPrice) : na
                    bool otherValid = not na(otherLevel) and (searchAbove ? otherLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : otherLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or otherDistance <= maximumDistance)
                    if otherValid and math.abs(otherLevel - candidateLevel) <= tolerance
                        clusterCount += 1
                        clusterScore += (otherIndex == 0 ? 24 : otherIndex == 1 ? 16 : otherIndex == 2 ? 12 : otherIndex <= 5 ? 5 : otherIndex <= 9 ? 3 : 1) + 4
            bool candidateWins = clusterScore > bestScore or clusterScore == bestScore and (clusterCount > bestCount or clusterCount == bestCount and (candidateIndex < bestRank or candidateIndex == bestRank and candidateDistance < bestDistance))
            if candidateWins
                bestLevel := candidateLevel
                bestScore := clusterScore
                bestCount := clusterCount
                bestRank := candidateIndex
                bestDistance := candidateDistance
    [bestLevel, bestScore, bestCount, bestRank]

// ترتيب موسع خاص بالأهداف يسمح بضم القنوات المقاسة وامتداد وتصحيح
// فيبوناتشي معاً من دون تغيير دوال الكلاستر الأخرى.
f_ranked_target_level(bool searchAbove, float currentPrice, float minimumDistance, float maximumDistance, float tolerance, array<float> levels) =>
    float bestLevel = na
    int bestScore = -1
    int bestCount = 0
    int bestRank = 99
    float bestDistance = 1e20
    int lastIndex = array.size(levels) - 1
    for candidateIndex = 0 to lastIndex
        float candidateLevel = array.get(levels, candidateIndex)
        float candidateDistance = not na(candidateLevel) ? math.abs(candidateLevel - currentPrice) : na
        bool candidateValid = not na(candidateLevel) and (searchAbove ? candidateLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : candidateLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or candidateDistance <= maximumDistance)
        if candidateValid
            int candidateWeight = candidateIndex == 0 ? 24 : candidateIndex == 1 ? 16 : candidateIndex == 2 ? 12 : candidateIndex <= 5 ? 5 : candidateIndex <= 9 ? 3 : 1
            int clusterCount = 1
            int clusterScore = candidateWeight
            for otherIndex = 0 to lastIndex
                if otherIndex != candidateIndex
                    float otherLevel = array.get(levels, otherIndex)
                    float otherDistance = not na(otherLevel) ? math.abs(otherLevel - currentPrice) : na
                    bool otherValid = not na(otherLevel) and (searchAbove ? otherLevel >= currentPrice + minimumDistance : otherLevel <= currentPrice - minimumDistance) and (na(maximumDistance) or otherDistance <= maximumDistance)
                    if otherValid and math.abs(otherLevel - candidateLevel) <= tolerance
                        int otherWeight = otherIndex == 0 ? 24 : otherIndex == 1 ? 16 : otherIndex == 2 ? 12 : otherIndex <= 5 ? 5 : otherIndex <= 9 ? 3 : 1
                        clusterCount += 1
                        clusterScore += otherWeight + 4
            bool candidateWins = clusterScore > bestScore or clusterScore == bestScore and (clusterCount > bestCount or clusterCount == bestCount and (candidateIndex < bestRank or candidateIndex == bestRank and candidateDistance < bestDistance))
            if candidateWins
                bestLevel := candidateLevel
                bestScore := clusterScore
                bestCount := clusterCount
                bestRank := candidateIndex
                bestDistance := candidateDistance
    [bestLevel, bestScore, bestCount, bestRank]

f_channel_signal(bool upperFakeout, bool lowerFakeout, bool channelIsReadySignal, bool breakoutUp, bool breakoutDown, int bias, float currentClose, float channelMiddle, bool trendIsReadySignal, bool supportBroken, bool resistanceBroken, float channelBase) =>
    upperFakeout ? -1 : lowerFakeout ? 1 : channelIsReadySignal ? (breakoutUp ? 1 : breakoutDown ? -1 : bias == 1 and currentClose >= channelMiddle ? 1 : bias == -1 and currentClose <= channelMiddle ? -1 : 0) : trendIsReadySignal ? (supportBroken ? -1 : resistanceBroken ? 1 : bias == 1 and currentClose >= channelBase ? 1 : bias == -1 and currentClose <= channelBase ? -1 : 0) : 0

// Merge the principal and secondary channel layers into one independent vote.
// a real disagreement produces no directional vote instead of double-counting.
f_new_chart_marker(int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float atrOffset) =>
    float safeMarkerLevel = na(markerLevel) ? close : markerLevel
    float safeMarkerAtr = na(markerAtr) ? syminfo.mintick * 10.0 : markerAtr
    // تبقى العلامة خارج جسم الشمعة بهامش ATR واضح، مع خلفية أخف حتى لا تحجب السعر.
    float markerOffset = math.max(safeMarkerAtr * math.max(atrOffset, 0.30), syminfo.mintick * 8.0)
    label.new(markerBar, below ? safeMarkerLevel - markerOffset : safeMarkerLevel + markerOffset, markerText, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = below ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down, color = color.new(markerColor, 38), textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny, force_overlay = true)

f_replace_chart_marker(label currentId, int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float atrOffset) =>
    f_clear_label(currentId)
    f_new_chart_marker(markerBar, markerLevel, markerText, below, markerColor, markerAtr, atrOffset)

f_push_chart_marker(array<label> labels, int maximumLabels, int markerBar, float markerLevel, string markerText, bool below, color markerColor, float markerAtr, float markerOffsetAtr) =>
    while array.size(labels) >= maximumLabels
        label oldestMarker = array.shift(labels)
        if not na(oldestMarker)
            label.delete(oldestMarker)
    // لكل فئة مسار ATR ثابت؛ وايكوف، الحدث، المراقبة والدخول تتكدس بلا تداخل.
    label marker = f_new_chart_marker(markerBar, markerLevel, markerText, below, markerColor, markerAtr, markerOffsetAtr)
    array.push(labels, marker)
    marker

f_close_plan_line(line lineId, int endBar, color baseColor, int transparency, int lineWidth) =>
    if not na(lineId)
        line.set_extend(lineId, extend.none)
        line.set_x2(lineId, endBar)
        line.set_color(lineId, color.new(baseColor, transparency))
        line.set_width(lineId, lineWidth)

f_close_plan_bundle(line entryId, line stopId, line target1Id, line target2Id, line target3Id, color entryColor, int entryTransparency, int stopTransparency, int targetTransparency) =>
    f_close_plan_line(entryId, bar_index, entryColor, entryTransparency, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(stopId, bar_index, #F23645, stopTransparency, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target1Id, bar_index, #22C55E, targetTransparency, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target2Id, bar_index, #22C55E, targetTransparency, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target3Id, bar_index, #22C55E, targetTransparency, 1)

f_project(float firstPrice, int firstBar, float secondPrice, int secondBar, int targetBar) =>
    bool validPoints = not na(firstPrice) and not na(secondPrice) and not na(firstBar) and not na(secondBar) and secondBar != firstBar
    validPoints ? secondPrice + (secondPrice - firstPrice) / float(secondBar - firstBar) * float(targetBar - secondBar) : na

// Count only separated reactions after the two defining anchors. A wick may
// enter the narrow trend zone, but the close must return to the valid side.
// Requiring price to leave the zone before another count prevents a cluster of
// adjacent candles from masquerading as several independent touches.
// A valid channel breakout has a classical measured objective equal to the
// perpendicular channel width. Keep only a fresh, still-unreached objective and
// expire it after a bounded number of chart bars.
f_update_channel_objectives(float currentBullTarget, int currentBullBar, float currentBearTarget, int currentBearBar, bool qualifiedBreakUp, bool qualifiedBreakDown, bool upperFakeout, bool lowerFakeout, bool geometryChanged, float upperBoundary, float lowerBoundary, float measuredWidth, int maximumAge) =>
    float nextBullTarget = currentBullTarget
    int nextBullBar = currentBullBar
    float nextBearTarget = currentBearTarget
    int nextBearBar = currentBearBar
    bool bullExpired = not na(nextBullBar) and bar_index - nextBullBar > maximumAge
    bool bearExpired = not na(nextBearBar) and bar_index - nextBearBar > maximumAge
    if geometryChanged
        nextBullTarget := na
        nextBullBar := na
        nextBearTarget := na
        nextBearBar := na
    if bullExpired or upperFakeout or qualifiedBreakDown or (not na(nextBullTarget) and high >= nextBullTarget)
        nextBullTarget := na
        nextBullBar := na
    if bearExpired or lowerFakeout or qualifiedBreakUp or (not na(nextBearTarget) and low <= nextBearTarget)
        nextBearTarget := na
        nextBearBar := na
    float bullCandidate = not na(upperBoundary) and not na(measuredWidth) ? upperBoundary + measuredWidth : na
    float bearCandidate = not na(lowerBoundary) and not na(measuredWidth) ? lowerBoundary - measuredWidth : na
    if qualifiedBreakUp and not na(bullCandidate) and bullCandidate > high + syminfo.mintick
        nextBullTarget := bullCandidate
        nextBullBar := bar_index
    if qualifiedBreakDown and not na(bearCandidate) and bearCandidate < low - syminfo.mintick and bearCandidate >= syminfo.mintick
        nextBearTarget := bearCandidate
        nextBearBar := bar_index
    [nextBullTarget, nextBullBar, nextBearTarget, nextBearBar]

// Pine resolves user-defined functions in declaration order. Keep this helper
// above the principal channel engine because that engine compares anchor IDs.
f_int_changed(int previousValue, int currentValue) =>
    na(previousValue) != na(currentValue) or (not na(previousValue) and not na(currentValue) and previousValue != currentValue)

// Maintain a second, slower set of confirmed pivots for the principal trend.
// returns only the latest valid principal trend and one parallel boundary, so
// the chart never accumulates a forest of historical lines.
f_update_major_channel(float pivotHighCandidate, float pivotLowCandidate, int candidateBar, int pivotStrength, int maximumValidationBars, float atrSeries, float breakAtrPart, float zoneAtrPart, float maximumProjectionAtr, float maximumSlopeAtr, float minimumWidthConsistency, int dowDirection, float closeBreakBuffer) =>
    var float previousHigh = na
    var float lastHigh = na
    var int previousHighBar = na
    var int lastHighBar = na
    var float previousLow = na
    var float lastLow = na
    var int previousLowBar = na
    var int lastLowBar = na
    var bool risingLineUnbroken = false
    var bool fallingLineUnbroken = false
    var bool risingLineInvalidated = false
    var bool fallingLineInvalidated = false

    bool newHigh = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(pivotHighCandidate)
    bool newLow = barstate.isconfirmed and not na(pivotLowCandidate)
    if newHigh
        previousHigh := lastHigh
        previousHighBar := lastHighBar
        lastHigh := pivotHighCandidate
        lastHighBar := candidateBar
    if newLow
        previousLow := lastLow
        previousLowBar := lastLowBar
        lastLow := pivotLowCandidate
        lastLowBar := candidateBar

    int minimumAnchorBars = pivotStrength + 1
    bool highsReady = not na(previousHigh) and not na(lastHigh) and not na(previousHighBar) and not na(lastHighBar) and lastHighBar - previousHighBar >= minimumAnchorBars
    bool lowsReady = not na(previousLow) and not na(lastLow) and not na(previousLowBar) and not na(lastLowBar) and lastLowBar - previousLowBar >= minimumAnchorBars
    float highSlope = highsReady ? (lastHigh - previousHigh) / float(lastHighBar - previousHighBar) : na
    float lowSlope = lowsReady ? (lastLow - previousLow) / float(lastLowBar - previousLowBar) : na

    if newLow
        risingLineInvalidated := false
        bool risingPair = lowsReady and lastLow > previousLow + syminfo.mintick
        risingLineUnbroken := risingPair
        if risingPair
            int validationSpan = bar_index - previousLowBar
            if validationSpan > maximumValidationBars
                risingLineUnbroken := false
            else
                for historyOffset = 0 to validationSpan
                    int historicalBar = bar_index - historyOffset
                    float lineAtHistory = f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, historicalBar)
                    float historicalAtr = nz(atrSeries[historyOffset], atrSeries)
                    float historicalBuffer = historicalAtr * math.max(breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart * 0.50)
                    if close[historyOffset] < lineAtHistory - historicalBuffer
                        risingLineUnbroken := false
                        break
    if newHigh
        fallingLineInvalidated := false
        bool fallingPair = highsReady and lastHigh < previousHigh - syminfo.mintick
        fallingLineUnbroken := fallingPair
        if fallingPair
            int validationSpan = bar_index - previousHighBar
            if validationSpan > maximumValidationBars
                fallingLineUnbroken := false
            else
                for historyOffset = 0 to validationSpan
                    int historicalBar = bar_index - historyOffset
                    float lineAtHistory = f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, historicalBar)
                    float historicalAtr = nz(atrSeries[historyOffset], atrSeries)
                    float historicalBuffer = historicalAtr * math.max(breakAtrPart, zoneAtrPart * 0.50)
                    if close[historyOffset] > lineAtHistory + historicalBuffer
                        fallingLineUnbroken := false
                        break

    bool risingAnchor = lowsReady and risingLineUnbroken and not risingLineInvalidated and lastLow > previousLow + syminfo.mintick
    bool fallingAnchor = highsReady and fallingLineUnbroken and not fallingLineInvalidated and lastHigh < previousHigh - syminfo.mintick
    bool risingStructure = highsReady and risingAnchor and lastHigh > previousHigh + syminfo.mintick
    bool fallingStructure = lowsReady and fallingAnchor and lastLow < previousLow - syminfo.mintick
    float risingWidth1 = risingStructure ? previousHigh - f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, previousHighBar) : na
    float risingWidth2 = risingStructure ? lastHigh - f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, lastHighBar) : na
    float fallingWidth1 = fallingStructure ? f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, previousLowBar) - previousLow : na
    float fallingWidth2 = fallingStructure ? f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, lastLowBar) - lastLow : na
    float majorRisingMaximumWidth = risingStructure ? math.max(risingWidth1, risingWidth2) : na
    float majorFallingMaximumWidth = fallingStructure ? math.max(fallingWidth1, fallingWidth2) : na
    float majorRisingMinimumWidth = risingStructure ? math.min(risingWidth1, risingWidth2) : na
    float majorFallingMinimumWidth = fallingStructure ? math.min(fallingWidth1, fallingWidth2) : na
    float risingConsistency = not na(majorRisingMaximumWidth) and majorRisingMaximumWidth > syminfo.mintick ? majorRisingMinimumWidth / majorRisingMaximumWidth : na
    float fallingConsistency = not na(majorFallingMaximumWidth) and majorFallingMaximumWidth > syminfo.mintick ? majorFallingMinimumWidth / majorFallingMaximumWidth : na
    bool risingChannel = risingStructure and majorRisingMinimumWidth > syminfo.mintick and risingConsistency >= minimumWidthConsistency
    bool fallingChannel = fallingStructure and majorFallingMinimumWidth > syminfo.mintick and fallingConsistency >= minimumWidthConsistency
    int completeBias = risingChannel and not fallingChannel ? 1 : fallingChannel and not risingChannel ? -1 : 0
    bool majorRisingTrendOnly = risingAnchor and not fallingAnchor and dowDirection != -1
    bool majorFallingTrendOnly = fallingAnchor and not risingAnchor and dowDirection != 1
    int majorTrendOnlyBias = majorRisingTrendOnly ? 1 : majorFallingTrendOnly ? -1 : 0
    int bias = completeBias != 0 ? completeBias : majorTrendOnlyBias
    float width = completeBias == 1 ? majorRisingMaximumWidth : completeBias == -1 ? majorFallingMaximumWidth : na
    float selectedSlope = bias == 1 ? lowSlope : bias == -1 ? highSlope : na
    float baseNow = bias == 1 ? f_project(previousLow, previousLowBar, lastLow, lastLowBar, bar_index) : bias == -1 ? f_project(previousHigh, previousHighBar, lastHigh, lastHighBar, bar_index) : na
    bool baseRelevant = not na(baseNow) and math.abs(close - baseNow) <= atrSeries * maximumProjectionAtr * 2.0
    bool slopeRelevant = not na(selectedSlope) and math.abs(selectedSlope) <= atrSeries * maximumSlopeAtr
    int selectedAnchorBar = bias == 1 ? lastLowBar : bias == -1 ? lastHighBar : na
    int maximumTrendAgeBars = math.min(maximumValidationBars, math.max(240, pivotStrength * 120))
    bool anchorFresh = not na(selectedAnchorBar) and bar_index - selectedAnchorBar <= maximumTrendAgeBars
    bool majorTrendReadyCalc = bias != 0 and baseRelevant and slopeRelevant and anchorFresh
    bool widthValid = not na(width) and width > syminfo.mintick and width <= atrSeries * maximumProjectionAtr * 2.0
    bool majorChannelReadyCalc = completeBias == bias and majorTrendReadyCalc and widthValid
    float lowerNow = majorChannelReadyCalc ? (bias == 1 ? baseNow : baseNow - width) : na
    float upperNow = majorChannelReadyCalc ? (bias == 1 ? baseNow + width : baseNow) : na
    int firstBar = bias == 1 ? previousLowBar : bias == -1 ? previousHighBar : na
    float firstPrice = bias == 1 ? previousLow : bias == -1 ? previousHigh : na
    int secondBar = bias == 1 ? lastLowBar : bias == -1 ? lastHighBar : na
    float secondPrice = bias == 1 ? lastLow : bias == -1 ? lastHigh : na
    // Persist line invalidation inside the function that owns the state.
    // The current confirmed break is still reported by the caller; from the next bar
    // the broken line stays disabled until a genuinely new anchor of that side appears.
    bool invalidateRisingNow = majorTrendReadyCalc and bias == 1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close < baseNow - closeBreakBuffer
    bool invalidateFallingNow = majorTrendReadyCalc and bias == -1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close > baseNow + closeBreakBuffer
    if invalidateRisingNow
        risingLineInvalidated := true
    if invalidateFallingNow
        fallingLineInvalidated := true
    [bias, majorTrendReadyCalc, majorChannelReadyCalc, baseNow, lowerNow, upperNow, width, firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice]

// compile-size optimization only; calculations and confirmation rules are the
// same as the secondary layer.
// Frozen trend-line retest: the anchors are saved on the break, so later pivots
// cannot move the watched level or repaint the retest.
// Frozen trend-line retest. Save the projected level and slope at the confirmed break,
// so later pivots cannot move the watched line or repaint the retest.
f_trendline_retest_state(bool trendIsReady, int trendBias, float trendBase, int firstAnchorBar, float firstAnchorPrice, int secondAnchorBar, float secondAnchorPrice, float closeBuffer, int retestBars, float atrValue, float closeLocationValue, float bodyRatioValue) =>
    bool anchorsReady = not na(firstAnchorBar) and not na(firstAnchorPrice) and not na(secondAnchorBar) and not na(secondAnchorPrice) and secondAnchorBar > firstAnchorBar
    bool stable = anchorsReady and trendIsReady and trendIsReady[1] and trendBias == trendBias[1] and not f_int_changed(firstAnchorBar[1], firstAnchorBar) and not f_int_changed(secondAnchorBar[1], secondAnchorBar)
    bool breakUp = stable and trendBias == -1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close > trendBase + closeBuffer and close[1] <= trendBase[1] + nz(closeBuffer[1], closeBuffer)
    bool breakDown = stable and trendBias == 1 and barstate.isconfirmed and close < trendBase - closeBuffer and close[1] >= trendBase[1] - nz(closeBuffer[1], closeBuffer)
    var int watchDirection = 0
    var int breakBar = na
    var float frozenBoundary = na
    var float frozenSlope = na
    int age = na(breakBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - breakBar
    float boundary = watchDirection != 0 and not na(frozenBoundary) and not na(frozenSlope) ? frozenBoundary + frozenSlope * age : na
    float tolerance = math.max(atrValue * math.max(trendZoneAtrWidth, retestAtrTolerance), syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float overshoot = math.max(tolerance * 2.5, syminfo.mintick * 8.0)
    bool bullRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and watchDirection == 1 and age > 0 and age <= retestBars and low <= boundary + tolerance and low >= boundary - overshoot and close > boundary + closeBuffer * 0.15 and close > open and closeLocationValue >= 0.60 and bodyRatioValue >= 0.35
    bool bearRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and watchDirection == -1 and age > 0 and age <= retestBars and high >= boundary - tolerance and high <= boundary + overshoot and close < boundary - closeBuffer * 0.15 and close < open and closeLocationValue <= 0.40 and bodyRatioValue >= 0.35
    bool invalid = watchDirection != 0 and (age > retestBars or watchDirection == 1 and close < boundary - overshoot or watchDirection == -1 and close > boundary + overshoot)
    if invalid or bullRetest or bearRetest
        watchDirection := 0
        breakBar := na
        frozenBoundary := na
        frozenSlope := na
    if breakUp or breakDown
        watchDirection := breakUp ? 1 : -1
        breakBar := bar_index
        frozenBoundary := trendBase
        frozenSlope := (secondAnchorPrice - firstAnchorPrice) / float(secondAnchorBar - firstAnchorBar)
    float eventBoundary = breakUp or breakDown ? trendBase : boundary
    [bullRetest, bearRetest, eventBoundary, breakUp, breakDown]

f_update_channel_visual(line trendLine, line parallelLine, bool showLayer, bool trendIsReady, bool channelIsReady, int bias, int firstBar, float firstPrice, int secondBar, float secondPrice, float channelWidthValue, color bullishColor, color bearishColor, int trendTransparency, int parallelTransparency, bool principalLayer) =>
    line nextTrendLine = trendLine
    line nextParallelLine = parallelLine
    bool anchorsReady = not na(firstBar) and not na(firstPrice) and not na(secondBar) and not na(secondPrice) and secondBar > firstBar
    bool directionReady = bias == 1 ? secondPrice > firstPrice + syminfo.mintick : bias == -1 ? secondPrice < firstPrice - syminfo.mintick : false
    if showLayer and trendIsReady and anchorsReady and directionReady
        color baseColor = bias == 1 ? bullishColor : bearishColor
        color trendColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? math.max(0, trendTransparency - 12) : math.min(95, trendTransparency + 10))
        color parallelColor = color.new(baseColor, principalLayer ? math.max(88, parallelTransparency) : math.max(94, parallelTransparency))
        string trendStyle = principalLayer ? line.style_solid : line.style_dotted
        string parallelStyle = principalLayer ? line.style_dashed : line.style_dotted
        int trendWidth = principalLayer ? (trendTransparency <= 35 ? 3 : 2) : 1
        if na(nextTrendLine)
            nextTrendLine := line.new(firstBar, firstPrice, secondBar, secondPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = trendColor, style = trendStyle, width = trendWidth, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(nextTrendLine, firstBar, firstPrice)
            line.set_xy2(nextTrendLine, secondBar, secondPrice)
            line.set_extend(nextTrendLine, extend.right)
            line.set_color(nextTrendLine, trendColor)
            line.set_style(nextTrendLine, trendStyle)
            line.set_width(nextTrendLine, trendWidth)
        if channelIsReady and not na(channelWidthValue)
            float parallelFirstPrice = bias == 1 ? firstPrice + channelWidthValue : firstPrice - channelWidthValue
            float parallelSecondPrice = bias == 1 ? secondPrice + channelWidthValue : secondPrice - channelWidthValue
            if na(nextParallelLine)
                nextParallelLine := line.new(firstBar, parallelFirstPrice, secondBar, parallelSecondPrice, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = parallelColor, style = parallelStyle, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
            else
                line.set_xy1(nextParallelLine, firstBar, parallelFirstPrice)
                line.set_xy2(nextParallelLine, secondBar, parallelSecondPrice)
                line.set_extend(nextParallelLine, extend.right)
                line.set_color(nextParallelLine, parallelColor)
                line.set_style(nextParallelLine, parallelStyle)
        else
            nextParallelLine := f_clear_line(nextParallelLine)
    else
        nextTrendLine := f_clear_line(nextTrendLine)
        nextParallelLine := f_clear_line(nextParallelLine)
    [nextTrendLine, nextParallelLine]

// Build one recent multi-touch cluster from pivots of the same type. Keeping
// highs and lows in separate arrays prevents a support and a resistance from
// being merged merely because their prices happen to be close.
f_strong_sr_cluster(array<float> prices, array<int> pivotBarIndexes, array<int> pivotTimes, float tolerance, int minimumTouches, int maximumAgeMilliseconds, int clusterPointLimit) =>
    float selectedLevel = na
    float selectedLow = na
    float selectedHigh = na
    int selectedTouches = 0
    int selectedRecentBar = na
    int availablePoints = math.min(math.min(array.size(prices), array.size(pivotBarIndexes)), math.min(array.size(pivotTimes), clusterPointLimit))
    if availablePoints > 0
        for candidateIndex = 0 to availablePoints - 1
            float candidatePrice = array.get(prices, candidateIndex)
            int candidateBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, candidateIndex)
            int candidateTime = array.get(pivotTimes, candidateIndex)
            if not na(candidateTime) and time >= candidateTime and time - candidateTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds
                float clusterSum = 0.0
                float clusterLow = candidatePrice
                float clusterHigh = candidatePrice
                int clusterTouches = 0
                int clusterRecentBar = candidateBar
                for pointIndex = 0 to availablePoints - 1
                    float pointPrice = array.get(prices, pointIndex)
                    int pointBar = array.get(pivotBarIndexes, pointIndex)
                    int pointTime = array.get(pivotTimes, pointIndex)
                    if not na(pointTime) and time >= pointTime and time - pointTime <= maximumAgeMilliseconds and math.abs(pointPrice - candidatePrice) <= tolerance
                        clusterSum += pointPrice
                        clusterLow := math.min(clusterLow, pointPrice)
                        clusterHigh := math.max(clusterHigh, pointPrice)
                        clusterTouches += 1
                        clusterRecentBar := math.max(clusterRecentBar, pointBar)
                bool betterCluster = clusterTouches > selectedTouches or (clusterTouches == selectedTouches and clusterRecentBar > nz(selectedRecentBar, -1))
                if betterCluster
                    selectedLevel := clusterSum / float(math.max(clusterTouches, 1))
                    selectedLow := clusterLow
                    selectedHigh := clusterHigh
                    selectedTouches := clusterTouches
                    selectedRecentBar := clusterRecentBar
    bool clusterQualified = selectedTouches >= minimumTouches
    [clusterQualified ? selectedLevel : na, clusterQualified ? selectedLow : na, clusterQualified ? selectedHigh : na, clusterQualified ? selectedTouches : 0, clusterQualified ? selectedRecentBar : na]

f_next_sr_expiry(array<int> pivotTimes, int maximumAgeMilliseconds) =>
    int nextExpiryTime = na
    int availableTimes = array.size(pivotTimes)
    if availableTimes > 0
        for pointIndex = 0 to availableTimes - 1
            int pointTime = array.get(pivotTimes, pointIndex)
            int pointExpiryTime = pointTime + maximumAgeMilliseconds + 1
            if pointExpiryTime > time
                nextExpiryTime := na(nextExpiryTime) ? pointExpiryTime : math.min(nextExpiryTime, pointExpiryTime)
    nextExpiryTime

f_float_changed(float previousValue, float currentValue) =>
    na(previousValue) != na(currentValue) or (not na(previousValue) and not na(currentValue) and math.abs(previousValue - currentValue) > syminfo.mintick * 0.50)

f_update_sr_line(line currentLine, int firstBar, float level, color lineColor, string lineStyle, int lineWidth, int transparency, bool extendBoth) =>
    line result = currentLine
    if na(level)
        result := f_clear_line(result)
    else
        int safeFirstBar = math.max(0, firstBar)
        int rightBar = bar_index + channelForwardBars
        string extension = extendBoth ? extend.both : extend.right
        if na(result)
            result := line.new(safeFirstBar, level, rightBar, level, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extension, color = color.new(lineColor, transparency), style = lineStyle, width = lineWidth, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(result, safeFirstBar, level)
            line.set_xy2(result, rightBar, level)
            line.set_extend(result, extension)
            line.set_color(result, color.new(lineColor, transparency))
            line.set_style(result, lineStyle)
            line.set_width(result, lineWidth)
    result

f_update_zone_visual(line currentTop, line currentBottom, linefill currentFill, bool enabled, int firstBar, int lastBar, float topLevel, float bottomLevel, color zoneColor) =>
    line topLine = currentTop
    line bottomLine = currentBottom
    linefill zoneFill = currentFill
    bool valid = enabled and not na(topLevel) and not na(bottomLevel) and topLevel > bottomLevel and lastBar > firstBar
    if not valid
        if not na(zoneFill)
            linefill.delete(zoneFill)
            zoneFill := na
        topLine := f_clear_line(topLine)
        bottomLine := f_clear_line(bottomLine)
    else
        int safeFirstBar = math.max(0, firstBar)
        if na(topLine)
            topLine := line.new(safeFirstBar, topLevel, lastBar, topLevel, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = color.new(zoneColor, 68), style = line.style_solid, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(topLine, safeFirstBar, topLevel)
            line.set_xy2(topLine, lastBar, topLevel)
            line.set_extend(topLine, extend.none)
            line.set_color(topLine, color.new(zoneColor, 68))
        if na(bottomLine)
            bottomLine := line.new(safeFirstBar, bottomLevel, lastBar, bottomLevel, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.none, color = color.new(zoneColor, 68), style = line.style_solid, width = 1, force_overlay = true)
        else
            line.set_xy1(bottomLine, safeFirstBar, bottomLevel)
            line.set_xy2(bottomLine, lastBar, bottomLevel)
            line.set_extend(bottomLine, extend.none)
            line.set_color(bottomLine, color.new(zoneColor, 68))
        if na(zoneFill)
            zoneFill := linefill.new(topLine, bottomLine, color.new(zoneColor, 92))
        else
            linefill.set_color(zoneFill, color.new(zoneColor, 92))
    [topLine, bottomLine, zoneFill]


f_sync_right_line_label(array<label> ids, int i, bool enabled, float level, string caption, color textColor, float atrValue) =>
    label id = array.get(ids, i)
    if not enabled or na(level)
        id := f_clear_label(id)
    else
        float y = level + math.max(syminfo.mintick * 4.0, math.max(nz(atrValue, 0.0), syminfo.mintick * 20.0) * (0.035 + float(i) * 0.012))
        string s = caption + "  " + str.tostring(level, format.mintick)
        if na(id)
            id := label.new(bar_index + channelForwardBars + 1, y, s, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = textColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left, force_overlay = true)
        else
            label.set_xy(id, bar_index + channelForwardBars + 1, y)
            label.set_text(id, s)
            label.set_textcolor(id, textColor)
    array.set(ids, i, id)

f_closed_htf_context() =>
    int monthMultiple = timeframe.ismonthly ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
    int fastLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 3 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 5 : 8) : timeframe.isweekly ? 13 : 20
    int slowLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 6 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 10 : 21) : timeframe.isweekly ? 34 : 50
    int contextLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? (monthMultiple >= 6 ? 5 : monthMultiple >= 3 ? 7 : 10) : timeframe.isweekly ? 10 : 14
    float fast = ta.ema(close, fastLength)
    float slow = ta.ema(close, slowLength)
    float momentum = ta.rsi(close, contextLength)
    float volatility = ta.atr(contextLength)
    bool ready = not na(slow[3]) and not na(momentum[1]) and not na(volatility[1]) and volatility[1] > 0
    float separation = ready ? (fast[1] - slow[1]) / volatility[1] : 0.0
    float slope = ready ? (fast[1] - fast[3]) / volatility[1] : 0.0
    int score = not ready ? 0 : (close[1] > fast[1] ? 1 : close[1] < fast[1] ? -1 : 0) + (separation >= 0.10 ? 1 : separation <= -0.10 ? -1 : 0) + (slope >= 0.05 ? 1 : slope <= -0.05 ? -1 : 0) + (momentum[1] >= 55 ? 1 : momentum[1] <= 45 ? -1 : 0)
    int trendState = not ready ? 9 : score >= 3 ? 2 : score >= 1 ? 1 : score <= -3 ? -2 : score <= -1 ? -1 : 0
    float confirmedPivotHighRaw = ta.pivothigh(high, 2, 2)
    float confirmedPivotLowRaw = ta.pivotlow(low, 2, 2)
    float confirmedPivotHigh = ta.valuewhen(not na(confirmedPivotHighRaw), confirmedPivotHighRaw, 0)[1]
    float confirmedPivotLow = ta.valuewhen(not na(confirmedPivotLowRaw), confirmedPivotLowRaw, 0)[1]
    [trendState, confirmedPivotHigh, confirmedPivotLow]

f_entry_evidence_pass(bool longSide, bool adaptive, bool directBreak, bool earlyQuality, float normalMinimum, float directMinimum, float earlyMinimum, float higherPenalty, int bullishCount, int bearishCount, float bullishPercent, float bearishPercent) =>
    int ownCount = longSide ? bullishCount : bearishCount
    int oppositeCount = longSide ? bearishCount : bullishCount
    float ownPercent = longSide ? bullishPercent : bearishPercent
    float requiredPercent = math.min(90.0, (not adaptive ? normalMinimum : directBreak ? directMinimum : earlyQuality ? earlyMinimum : normalMinimum) + higherPenalty)
    ownPercent >= requiredPercent and ownCount >= oppositeCount + (adaptive and directBreak ? 2 : 1)

f_independent_consensus_pass(bool earlyEvent, int ownAxes, int oppositeAxes, float ownPercent, float oppositePercent, int ownCount, int oppositeCount, float strongMinimum) =>
    bool strongSingle = not earlyEvent and ownPercent >= strongMinimum and ownCount >= oppositeCount + 2
    (ownAxes >= 2 or strongSingle) and not (oppositeAxes >= 2 and oppositePercent >= ownPercent * 0.86)

f_exceptional_direct_break_pass(bool longSide, bool active, bool directBreak, bool qualified, bool participationPass, float bodyRatioValue, float closeLocationValue, int ownAxes, int ownCount, int oppositeCount, int higherScore, bool reversalBreak) =>
    bool closeQuality = longSide ? closeLocationValue >= 0.72 : closeLocationValue <= 0.28
    bool higherAligned = longSide ? higherScore >= 0 or reversalBreak and higherScore >= -2 : higherScore <= 0 or reversalBreak and higherScore <= 2
    active and directBreak and qualified and participationPass and bodyRatioValue >= 0.55 and closeQuality and ownAxes >= 2 and ownCount >= oppositeCount + 2 and higherAligned

// تحليل الأسهم يعتمد على سعر وحجم الأصل نفسه فقط.
bool stockSymbolAllowed = syminfo.type == "stock" or syminfo.type == "fund" or syminfo.type == "dr"
int chartSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds())
int minimumSupportedSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds("D"))
int weeklyTimeframeSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds("W"))
int monthlyTimeframeSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds("M"))
int maximumSupportedSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds("M"))
bool supportedTimeframe = chartSeconds >= minimumSupportedSeconds and chartSeconds <= maximumSupportedSeconds
bool standardChartAllowed = chart.is_standard  // الشموع العادية والمفرغة تستخدم OHLC الحقيقي؛ Heikin Ashi والأنواع الصناعية غير معتمدة للتنفيذ.
bool stockChartAllowed = stockSymbolAllowed and supportedTimeframe and standardChartAllowed
// إشارات الشارت مصدرها الفاصل المفتوح فقط. الفواصل الأعلى للرأي المترابط والجدول ولا يرسمان أحداثا على الشموع.
bool chartSignalDisplayAllowed = stockChartAllowed

// Apply a moderate additional horizon factor instead of multiplying time twice.
// target horizons without changing the structural stop logic.
bool adaptiveTargetsEnabled = targetAdaptationMode == TARGET_AUTO
float automaticTargetHorizonFactor = timeframe.isdaily ? 1.25 : timeframe.isweekly ? 1.50 : timeframe.multiplier >= 3 ? 2.00 : 1.80
float targetHorizonFactor = adaptiveTargetsEnabled ? automaticTargetHorizonFactor : 1.0
// هذه النسخة تستخدم العرض التفصيلي فقط؛ لا تنفذ أي فروع خاصة بالفواصل اللحظية.
int visualPlanBoxOffsetBars = math.max(planBoxOffsetBars, 7)
// their nearer offset, while entry/stop/target captions use three times the distance.
int visualExplanationOffsetBars = visualPlanBoxOffsetBars * 3

f_new_plan_level_label(float level, color textColor) =>
    label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, level, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = textColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left, force_overlay = true)

f_replace_plan_level_label(label currentId, bool enabled, float level, color textColor) =>
    f_clear_label(currentId)
    enabled ? f_new_plan_level_label(level, textColor) : na
int trendLineTransparency = 62
int channelLineTransparency = 84
int wyckoffRangeLineTransparency = 68
int planEntryLineTransparency = 5
int planStopLineTransparency = 5
int planTarget1LineTransparency = 15
int planTarget2LineTransparency = 20
int planTarget3LineTransparency = 25

if barstate.isfirst and not supportedTimeframe
    runtime.error("الفاصل المدعوم: يومي إلى شهري")
if barstate.isfirst and not standardChartAllowed
    runtime.error("استخدم شموعًا عادية")
if barstate.isfirst and not stockSymbolAllowed
    runtime.error("المؤشر للأسهم والصناديق فقط")
// قاعدة 2+2 هي الحد الأدنى الكلاسيكي للمحور الصحيح. يستطيع المستخدم رفع
// القوة يدويا للحصول على قمم وقيعان هيكلية أبطأ وأقل عددا.
int autoPivotBars = 2
int pivotBars = swingMode == AUTO_MODE ? autoPivotBars : manualPivotBars
// محاور الرسم أقوى من محاور بنية 2+2. تتسع تلقائيا على الفواصل السريعة
// لتناسب اليومي والأسبوعي والشهري دون تأخير مفرط.
int channelPivotBars = swingMode == MANUAL_MODE ? math.max(manualPivotBars, 3) : chartSeconds < monthlyTimeframeSeconds ? 4 : 3
int majorChannelPivotBars = math.min(20, math.max(channelPivotBars + 2, int(math.round(float(channelPivotBars) * 1.50))))
int chartMonthMultiple = timeframe.ismonthly ? timeframe.multiplier : 0
string higherTimeframe1 = chartSeconds < weeklyTimeframeSeconds ? "W" : chartSeconds < monthlyTimeframeSeconds ? "M" : chartMonthMultiple < 3 ? "3M" : "4M"
string higherTimeframe2 = chartSeconds < weeklyTimeframeSeconds ? "M" : chartSeconds < monthlyTimeframeSeconds ? "3M" : "6M"
string higherTimeframe3 = chartSeconds < weeklyTimeframeSeconds ? "3M" : chartSeconds < monthlyTimeframeSeconds ? "6M" : "12M"
// اليومي والأسبوعي والشهري يراجع ثلاثة سياقات أعلى مكتملة.
[higherTrendRaw1, higherResistance1, higherSupport1] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe1, f_closed_htf_context(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on, calc_bars_count = 800)
[higherTrendRaw2, higherResistance2, higherSupport2] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe2, f_closed_htf_context(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on, calc_bars_count = 800)
[higherTrendRaw3, higherResistance3, higherSupport3] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, higherTimeframe3, f_closed_htf_context(), lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on, calc_bars_count = 800)
float higherTimeframeResistance = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, higherResistance1, higherResistance2, higherResistance3, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float higherTimeframeSupport = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, higherSupport1, higherSupport2, higherSupport3, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float priorHigherTimeframeResistance = f_nearest_price_level(true, nz(close[1], close), syminfo.mintick * 2.0, higherResistance1, higherResistance2, higherResistance3, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
float priorHigherTimeframeSupport = f_nearest_price_level(false, nz(close[1], close), syminfo.mintick * 2.0, higherSupport1, higherSupport2, higherSupport3, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
f_higher_context_summary(int raw1, int raw2, int raw3) =>
    int state1 = raw1 == 9 ? 0 : raw1
    int state2 = raw2 == 9 ? 0 : raw2
    int state3 = raw3 == 9 ? 0 : raw3
    bool sufficient = (raw1 != 9 ? 1 : 0) + (raw2 != 9 ? 1 : 0) + (raw3 != 9 ? 1 : 0) >= 2
    int weightedScore = state1 * 3 + state2 * 2 + state3
    float longPenalty = requireHigherTimeframeAlignment ? math.min(10.0, float(math.max(0, -weightedScore))) : 0.0
    float shortPenalty = requireHigherTimeframeAlignment ? math.min(10.0, float(math.max(0, weightedScore))) : 0.0
    [state1, state2, state3, sufficient, weightedScore, longPenalty, shortPenalty]

[_, _, _, higherContextsSufficient, higherWeightedScore, higherLongPenalty, higherShortPenalty] = f_higher_context_summary(higherTrendRaw1, higherTrendRaw2, higherTrendRaw3)
bool higherTimeframesReady = higherContextsSufficient

// Otherwise an anchor equal to the chart interval resets every bar and
// degenerates into the bar's own typical price. Monthly uses 3M.
string automaticVwapAnchorTimeframe = chartSeconds < monthlyTimeframeSeconds ? "M" : chartMonthMultiple < 3 ? "3M" : "6M"
string requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe = vwapAnchorMode == ANCHOR_DAY ? "D" : vwapAnchorMode == ANCHOR_WEEK ? "W" : vwapAnchorMode == ANCHOR_MONTH ? "M" : automaticVwapAnchorTimeframe
int requestedVwapAnchorSeconds = int(timeframe.in_seconds(requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe))
string effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe = requestedVwapAnchorSeconds > chartSeconds ? requestedVwapAnchorTimeframe : higherTimeframe1
bool selectedVwapAnchor = barstate.isfirst or timeframe.change(effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe)

// =============================================================================
// Core calculations and optional filters
// =============================================================================
float atrValue = ta.atr(atrLength)
float safeAtr = nz(atrValue, math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick))
// Independent plan-management implementation of a volatility trail. These
// series are calculated globally on every bar, but never create an entry.
float postTargetTrailAtr = ta.atr(postTargetTrailLength)
// ta.lowest over a fixed window could import a pre-entry extreme and jump the
// trail unrealistically close to price immediately after T1.
bool postTargetTrailReady = not na(postTargetTrailAtr)
float maFast = ta.sma(close, 20)
float maMiddle = ta.sma(close, 100)
float maSlow = ta.sma(close, 200)
float ema50 = ta.ema(close, 50)
float ema20 = ta.ema(close, 20)
int movingAverageBias = not na(maSlow) and not na(ema50) ? close > maFast and maFast > ema50 and ema50 > maMiddle and maMiddle > maSlow ? 1 : close < maFast and maFast < ema50 and ema50 < maMiddle and maMiddle < maSlow ? -1 : 0 : 0
float rsiValue = ta.rsi(close, rsiLength)
float macdLine = ta.ema(close, 12) - ta.ema(close, 26)
float macdSignalLine = ta.ema(macdLine, 9)
[plusDi, minusDi, adxValue] = ta.dmi(14, 14)

// EMA/RSI/MACD are merged later into one bounded recovery vote, not three
// independent votes, preventing duplicated momentum weighting.
int adaptiveMaFastLength = timeframe.isdaily ? 50 : timeframe.isweekly ? 10 : 3
int adaptiveMaSlowLength = timeframe.isdaily ? 200 : timeframe.isweekly ? 40 : 10
f_vidya_center() =>
    float delta = ta.change(close)
    float positiveMomentum = math.sum(math.max(delta, 0.0), adaptiveMaSlowLength)
    float negativeMomentum = math.sum(math.max(-delta, 0.0), adaptiveMaSlowLength)
    float totalMomentum = positiveMomentum + negativeMomentum
    float efficiency = totalMomentum > 0 ? math.abs(positiveMomentum - negativeMomentum) / totalMomentum : 0.0
    float alpha = 2.0 / (float(adaptiveMaFastLength) + 1.0) * efficiency
    var float value = na
    value := na(value[1]) ? close : alpha * close + (1.0 - alpha) * value[1]
    value


float adaptiveTrendCenter = f_vidya_center()
float adaptiveTrendBand = safeAtr * 1.50
var int adaptiveTrendBias = 0
if barstate.isconfirmed and not na(adaptiveTrendCenter)
    adaptiveTrendBias := close > adaptiveTrendCenter + adaptiveTrendBand ? 1 : close < adaptiveTrendCenter - adaptiveTrendBand ? -1 : adaptiveTrendBias


// Daily and higher charts compare volume only with prior closed bars.
float rollingVolumeAverage = ta.sma(volume, volumeLength)[1]
float volumeAverage = rollingVolumeAverage
bool volumeAvailable = not na(volume) and not na(volumeAverage) and volumeAverage > 0
float relativeVolume = volumeAvailable ? volume / volumeAverage : na
int volumeRegimeLength = timeframe.ismonthly ? 24 : timeframe.isweekly ? 52 : math.max(30, volumeLength * 5)
float volumeRegimeMean = ta.sma(relativeVolume[1], volumeRegimeLength)
float volumeRegimeDeviation = ta.stdev(relativeVolume[1], volumeRegimeLength)
float volumeZScore = volumeAvailable and not na(volumeRegimeMean) and volumeRegimeDeviation > 0 ? (relativeVolume - volumeRegimeMean) / volumeRegimeDeviation : na
bool statisticalVolumeHigh = not na(volumeZScore) and volumeZScore >= 1.0
bool statisticalVolumeExtreme = not na(volumeZScore) and volumeZScore >= 2.0
// طبقات الحجم مبنية على Z-Score للحجم النسبي نفسه. لحظيًا يحافظ ذلك على
// مقارنة وقت الجلسة المماثل بدل تضخيم افتتاح وإغلاق السوق بسبب الحجم الخام.
float heatmapVolumeZ = volumeZScore
int heatmapVolumeTier = na(heatmapVolumeZ) ? 0 : heatmapVolumeZ > 4.0 ? 5 : heatmapVolumeZ > 2.5 ? 4 : heatmapVolumeZ > 1.0 ? 3 : heatmapVolumeZ > -0.5 ? 2 : 1
bool heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher = heatmapVolumeTier >= 3
bool heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher = heatmapVolumeTier >= 4
bool heatmapVolumeExtreme = heatmapVolumeTier >= 5
bool standardVolumeEffort = relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeMediumOrHigher
bool volumePass = not useVolumeFilter or (volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort)
float obvValue = ta.cum(math.sign(nz(ta.change(close), 0.0)) * nz(volume, 0.0))
float obvAverage = ta.ema(obvValue, 21)
float obvSlope = obvValue - nz(obvValue[5], obvValue)
int obvTrendSignal = not volumeAvailable ? 0 : obvValue > obvAverage and obvSlope > 0 ? 1 : obvValue < obvAverage and obvSlope < 0 ? -1 : 0

float vwapValue = ta.vwap(hlc3, selectedVwapAnchor)
// Structural VWAP is anchored by events calculated later in the script. The
// entry gate therefore uses its last confirmed value, avoiding a circular same-
// bar dependency. Until the first structural anchor is ready, periodic VWAP is
// the explicit fallback.
var float confirmedAnalysisVwapValue = na
var bool confirmedStructuralVwapReady = false
float entryVwapValue = useStructuralVwap and confirmedStructuralVwapReady and not na(confirmedAnalysisVwapValue) ? confirmedAnalysisVwapValue : vwapValue
bool vwapLongPass = not useVwapFilter or (not na(entryVwapValue) and close > entryVwapValue)
bool vwapShortPass = not useVwapFilter or (not na(entryVwapValue) and close < entryVwapValue)

f_update_fvg_state() =>
    var bool bullishFvgActive = false
    var bool bearishFvgActive = false
    var float bullishFvgBottom = na
    var float bearishFvgTop = na
    var int bullishFvgBar = na
    var int bearishFvgBar = na
    bool fvgBullPass = not useFvgFilter
    bool fvgBearPass = not useFvgFilter
    bool recentBullFvg = false
    bool recentBearFvg = false
    float middleCandleRange = math.max(high[1] - low[1], syminfo.mintick)
    float middleCandleBodyRatio = math.abs(close[1] - open[1]) / middleCandleRange
    bool fvgDisplacementVolumePass = not confirmFvgDisplacement or (volumeAvailable[1] and relativeVolume[1] >= volumeMultiple)
    bool fvgDisplacementBodyPass = not confirmFvgDisplacement or middleCandleBodyRatio >= 0.55
    bool bullishFvg = bar_index >= 2 and low > high[2] and close[1] > open[1] and fvgDisplacementVolumePass and fvgDisplacementBodyPass
    bool bearishFvg = bar_index >= 2 and high < low[2] and close[1] < open[1] and fvgDisplacementVolumePass and fvgDisplacementBodyPass

    if bullishFvg
        bullishFvgActive := true
        bullishFvgBottom := high[2]
        bullishFvgBar := bar_index
    if bearishFvg
        bearishFvgActive := true
        bearishFvgTop := low[2]
        bearishFvgBar := bar_index

    // الفجوة تبقى صالحة فقط ما دامت حديثة ولم تُملأ بالكامل بعودة السعر إلى حدها البعيد.
    bool bullishFvgFilled = bullishFvgActive and not na(bullishFvgBar) and bar_index > bullishFvgBar and low <= bullishFvgBottom
    bool bearishFvgFilled = bearishFvgActive and not na(bearishFvgBar) and bar_index > bearishFvgBar and high >= bearishFvgTop
    if bullishFvgFilled or (bullishFvgActive and bar_index - bullishFvgBar > fvgLookback)
        bullishFvgActive := false
        bullishFvgBottom := na
        bullishFvgBar := na
    if bearishFvgFilled or (bearishFvgActive and bar_index - bearishFvgBar > fvgLookback)
        bearishFvgActive := false
        bearishFvgTop := na
        bearishFvgBar := na

    int barsSinceBullFvg = bullishFvgActive and not na(bullishFvgBar) ? bar_index - bullishFvgBar : 100000
    int barsSinceBearFvg = bearishFvgActive and not na(bearishFvgBar) ? bar_index - bearishFvgBar : 100000
    recentBullFvg := bullishFvgActive and barsSinceBullFvg <= fvgLookback
    recentBearFvg := bearishFvgActive and barsSinceBearFvg <= fvgLookback
    fvgBullPass := not useFvgFilter or recentBullFvg
    fvgBearPass := not useFvgFilter or recentBearFvg

    [bullishFvgActive, bearishFvgActive, bullishFvgBottom, bearishFvgTop, bullishFvgBar, bearishFvgBar, fvgBullPass, fvgBearPass, recentBullFvg, recentBearFvg]

[bullishFvgActive, bearishFvgActive, bullishFvgBottom, bearishFvgTop, bullishFvgBar, bearishFvgBar, fvgBullPass, fvgBearPass, recentBullFvg, recentBearFvg] = f_update_fvg_state()
float candleRange = math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick)
float bodyRatio = math.abs(close - open) / candleRange
float averageSpread = ta.sma(candleRange, volumeLength)
float relativeSpread = not na(averageSpread) and averageSpread > 0 ? candleRange / averageSpread : na
float closeLocation = (close - low) / candleRange
bool vsaHighEffort = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable and standardVolumeEffort
bool vsaWideResult = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread >= 1.20
bool vsaNarrowResult = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread <= 0.75
bool bullishVsaEffortResult = vsaHighEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.65
bool bearishVsaEffortResult = vsaHighEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.35

// =============================================================================
// Dow structure: every strength uses a strict symmetric pivot. The candidate
// must be strictly above/below every candle on both sides; equal highs/lows do
// not become separate pivots. Only the latest confirmed pair is shown.
// =============================================================================
f_confirm_symmetric_pivots() =>
    float pivotHigh = na
    float pivotLow = na
    bool newPivotHigh = false
    bool newPivotLow = false
    float structureRightHigh = ta.highest(high, pivotBars)
    float structureLeftHigh = ta.highest(high[pivotBars + 1], pivotBars)
    float structureRightLow = ta.lowest(low, pivotBars)
    float structureLeftLow = ta.lowest(low[pivotBars + 1], pivotBars)
    bool strictPivotHigh = bar_index >= pivotBars * 2 and high[pivotBars] > structureRightHigh and high[pivotBars] > structureLeftHigh
    bool strictPivotLow = bar_index >= pivotBars * 2 and low[pivotBars] < structureRightLow and low[pivotBars] < structureLeftLow
    bool ambiguousStructurePivot = strictPivotHigh and strictPivotLow
    pivotHigh := strictPivotHigh and not ambiguousStructurePivot ? high[pivotBars] : na
    pivotLow := strictPivotLow and not ambiguousStructurePivot ? low[pivotBars] : na
    newPivotHigh := stockChartAllowed and not na(pivotHigh) and barstate.isconfirmed
    newPivotLow := stockChartAllowed and not na(pivotLow) and barstate.isconfirmed

    [pivotHigh, pivotLow, newPivotHigh, newPivotLow]

[pivotHigh, pivotLow, newPivotHigh, newPivotLow] = f_confirm_symmetric_pivots()
var float lastSwingHigh = na
var float lastSwingLow = na
var int lastSwingHighBar = na
var int lastSwingLowBar = na
var float lastPivotHighRsi = na
var float lastPivotLowRsi = na
var float lastPivotHighObv = na
var float lastPivotLowObv = na
var string lastHighTag = "-"
var string lastLowTag = "-"
var bool highWasBroken = false
var bool lowWasBroken = false
var bool highWasSwept = false
var bool lowWasSwept = false

var bool doubleTopArmed = false
var bool doubleBottomArmed = false
var float doubleTopNeckline = na
var float doubleBottomNeckline = na
var float doubleTopPrice = na
var float doubleBottomPrice = na
var float doubleTopHeight = na
var float doubleBottomHeight = na
var int doubleTopArmedBar = na
var int doubleBottomArmedBar = na

var array<float> srHighPivotPrices = array.new<float>()
var array<int> srHighPivotBars = array.new<int>()
var array<int> srHighPivotTimes = array.new<int>()
var array<bool> srHighPivotBroken = array.new<bool>()
var array<float> srLowPivotPrices = array.new<float>()
var array<int> srLowPivotBars = array.new<int>()
var array<int> srLowPivotTimes = array.new<int>()
var array<bool> srLowPivotBroken = array.new<bool>()
var float strongResistanceLevel = na
var float strongResistanceLow = na
var float strongResistanceHigh = na
var int strongResistanceTouches = 0
var int strongResistanceRecentBar = na
var float strongSupportLevel = na
var float strongSupportLow = na
var float strongSupportHigh = na
var int strongSupportTouches = 0
var int strongSupportRecentBar = na

bool bearishRsiDivergence = false
bool bullishRsiDivergence = false
bool bearishObvDivergence = false
bool bullishObvDivergence = false
var int obvDivergenceState = 0
var int obvDivergenceExpiryBar = na
var float obvDivergenceInvalidation = na

float pivotTolerance = nz(atrValue[pivotBars], safeAtr) * equalAtrPart
float patternTolerance = math.max(pivotTolerance, nz(atrValue[pivotBars], safeAtr) * 0.25)

if newPivotHigh
    int pivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
    float confirmedPivotRsi = rsiValue[pivotBars]
    float confirmedPivotObv = obvValue[pivotBars]
    array.unshift(srHighPivotPrices, pivotHigh)
    array.unshift(srHighPivotBars, pivotBar)
    array.unshift(srHighPivotTimes, time[pivotBars])
    array.unshift(srHighPivotBroken, false)
    while array.size(srHighPivotPrices) > srHistoricalArchiveLimit
        array.pop(srHighPivotPrices)
        array.pop(srHighPivotBars)
        array.pop(srHighPivotTimes)
        array.pop(srHighPivotBroken)
    bearishRsiDivergence := useRsiDivergence and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastPivotHighRsi) and pivotHigh >= lastSwingHigh - pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotRsi <= lastPivotHighRsi - rsiDivergenceGap
    bearishObvDivergence := volumeAvailable and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastPivotHighObv) and pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotObv < lastPivotHighObv
    bool validDoubleTop = useChartPatterns and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and lastSwingLowBar > lastSwingHighBar and lastSwingLowBar < pivotBar and math.abs(pivotHigh - lastSwingHigh) <= patternTolerance and math.min(pivotHigh, lastSwingHigh) - lastSwingLow >= safeAtr
    if validDoubleTop
        doubleTopArmed := true
        doubleTopArmedBar := pivotBar
        doubleTopNeckline := lastSwingLow
        doubleTopPrice := (pivotHigh + lastSwingHigh) * 0.50
        doubleTopHeight := doubleTopPrice - doubleTopNeckline
    else if not na(lastSwingHigh) and pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + patternTolerance
        doubleTopArmed := false
        doubleTopArmedBar := na
        doubleTopNeckline := na
        doubleTopPrice := na
        doubleTopHeight := na
    string highTag = na(lastSwingHigh) ? "H" : pivotHigh > lastSwingHigh + pivotTolerance ? "HH" : pivotHigh < lastSwingHigh - pivotTolerance ? "LH" : "EH"
    lastSwingHigh := pivotHigh
    lastSwingHighBar := pivotBar
    lastPivotHighRsi := confirmedPivotRsi
    lastPivotHighObv := confirmedPivotObv
    lastHighTag := highTag
    highWasBroken := false
    highWasSwept := false


if newPivotLow
    int pivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
    float confirmedPivotRsi = rsiValue[pivotBars]
    float confirmedPivotObv = obvValue[pivotBars]
    array.unshift(srLowPivotPrices, pivotLow)
    array.unshift(srLowPivotBars, pivotBar)
    array.unshift(srLowPivotTimes, time[pivotBars])
    array.unshift(srLowPivotBroken, false)
    while array.size(srLowPivotPrices) > srHistoricalArchiveLimit
        array.pop(srLowPivotPrices)
        array.pop(srLowPivotBars)
        array.pop(srLowPivotTimes)
        array.pop(srLowPivotBroken)
    bullishRsiDivergence := useRsiDivergence and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastPivotLowRsi) and pivotLow <= lastSwingLow + pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotRsi >= lastPivotLowRsi + rsiDivergenceGap
    bullishObvDivergence := volumeAvailable and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastPivotLowObv) and pivotLow < lastSwingLow - pivotTolerance and confirmedPivotObv > lastPivotLowObv
    bool validDoubleBottom = useChartPatterns and not na(lastSwingLow) and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLowBar) and not na(lastSwingHighBar) and lastSwingHighBar > lastSwingLowBar and lastSwingHighBar < pivotBar and math.abs(pivotLow - lastSwingLow) <= patternTolerance and lastSwingHigh - math.max(pivotLow, lastSwingLow) >= safeAtr
    if validDoubleBottom
        doubleBottomArmed := true
        doubleBottomArmedBar := pivotBar
        doubleBottomNeckline := lastSwingHigh
        doubleBottomPrice := (pivotLow + lastSwingLow) * 0.50
        doubleBottomHeight := doubleBottomNeckline - doubleBottomPrice
    else if not na(lastSwingLow) and pivotLow < lastSwingLow - patternTolerance
        doubleBottomArmed := false
        doubleBottomArmedBar := na
        doubleBottomNeckline := na
        doubleBottomPrice := na
        doubleBottomHeight := na
    string lowTag = na(lastSwingLow) ? "L" : pivotLow > lastSwingLow + pivotTolerance ? "HL" : pivotLow < lastSwingLow - pivotTolerance ? "LL" : "EL"
    lastSwingLow := pivotLow
    lastSwingLowBar := pivotBar
    lastPivotLowRsi := confirmedPivotRsi
    lastPivotLowObv := confirmedPivotObv
    lastLowTag := lowTag
    lowWasBroken := false
    lowWasSwept := false

int obvDivergenceValidityBars = math.min(30, math.max(6, pivotBars * 5))
if barstate.isconfirmed
    if bearishObvDivergence
        obvDivergenceState := -1
        obvDivergenceExpiryBar := bar_index + obvDivergenceValidityBars
        obvDivergenceInvalidation := pivotHigh
    else if bullishObvDivergence
        obvDivergenceState := 1
        obvDivergenceExpiryBar := bar_index + obvDivergenceValidityBars
        obvDivergenceInvalidation := pivotLow
    else if not volumeAvailable or (obvDivergenceState == -1 and (bar_index > nz(obvDivergenceExpiryBar, bar_index) or close > nz(obvDivergenceInvalidation, close) + pivotTolerance)) or (obvDivergenceState == 1 and (bar_index > nz(obvDivergenceExpiryBar, bar_index) or close < nz(obvDivergenceInvalidation, close) - pivotTolerance))
        obvDivergenceState := 0
        obvDivergenceExpiryBar := na
        obvDivergenceInvalidation := na
bool recentBearishObvDivergence = obvDivergenceState == -1
bool recentBullishObvDivergence = obvDivergenceState == 1
int obvSignal = recentBearishObvDivergence and obvTrendSignal == 1 or recentBullishObvDivergence and obvTrendSignal == -1 ? 0 : obvTrendSignal


// Recalculate on a new confirmed pivot or exactly when a stored touch ages out.
// price-clustering loops on every bar.
var int nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime = na
bool horizontalSrAgeRefresh = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and not na(nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime) and time >= nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime
bool horizontalSrRecalculated = useHorizontalSr and barstate.isconfirmed and (newPivotHigh or newPivotLow or horizontalSrAgeRefresh)
bool strongResistanceClusterChanged = false
bool strongSupportClusterChanged = false
if horizontalSrRecalculated
    float horizontalSrTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * srAtrTolerance, syminfo.mintick * 5.0)
    [resistanceLevelCandidate, resistanceLowCandidate, resistanceHighCandidate, resistanceTouchesCandidate, resistanceRecentBarCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srHighPivotPrices, srHighPivotBars, srHighPivotTimes, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, srStoredPivots)
    [supportLevelCandidate, supportLowCandidate, supportHighCandidate, supportTouchesCandidate, supportRecentBarCandidate] = f_strong_sr_cluster(srLowPivotPrices, srLowPivotBars, srLowPivotTimes, horizontalSrTolerance, srMinimumTouches, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds, srStoredPivots)
    strongResistanceClusterChanged := f_float_changed(strongResistanceLevel, resistanceLevelCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongResistanceLow, resistanceLowCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongResistanceHigh, resistanceHighCandidate) or strongResistanceTouches != resistanceTouchesCandidate or f_int_changed(strongResistanceRecentBar, resistanceRecentBarCandidate)
    strongSupportClusterChanged := f_float_changed(strongSupportLevel, supportLevelCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongSupportLow, supportLowCandidate) or f_float_changed(strongSupportHigh, supportHighCandidate) or strongSupportTouches != supportTouchesCandidate or f_int_changed(strongSupportRecentBar, supportRecentBarCandidate)
    strongResistanceLevel := resistanceLevelCandidate
    strongResistanceLow := resistanceLowCandidate
    strongResistanceHigh := resistanceHighCandidate
    strongResistanceTouches := resistanceTouchesCandidate
    strongResistanceRecentBar := resistanceRecentBarCandidate
    strongSupportLevel := supportLevelCandidate
    strongSupportLow := supportLowCandidate
    strongSupportHigh := supportHighCandidate
    strongSupportTouches := supportTouchesCandidate
    strongSupportRecentBar := supportRecentBarCandidate
    int nextResistanceExpiryTime = f_next_sr_expiry(srHighPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    int nextSupportExpiryTime = f_next_sr_expiry(srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    nextHorizontalSrExpiryTime := na(nextResistanceExpiryTime) ? nextSupportExpiryTime : na(nextSupportExpiryTime) ? nextResistanceExpiryTime : math.min(nextResistanceExpiryTime, nextSupportExpiryTime)

bool strongResistanceExpired = horizontalSrAgeRefresh and strongResistanceClusterChanged and na(strongResistanceLevel)
bool strongSupportExpired = horizontalSrAgeRefresh and strongSupportClusterChanged and na(strongSupportLevel)
if strongResistanceExpired
    strongResistanceLevel := na
    strongResistanceLow := na
    strongResistanceHigh := na
    strongResistanceTouches := 0
    strongResistanceRecentBar := na
