    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 3, stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not na(cleanSupportLevel2), cleanSupportLevel2, "الدعم التالي", color.new(#F59E0B, 15), safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 4, stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not cleanChartMode and not na(priorHigherTimeframeResistance), priorHigherTimeframeResistance, "مقاومة HTF", bearColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 5, stockChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not cleanChartMode and not na(priorHigherTimeframeSupport), priorHigherTimeframeSupport, "دعم HTF", bullColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 6, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and not cleanChartMode and bullishBreakerActive, bullishBreakerActive ? (bullishBreakerHigh + bullishBreakerLow) * 0.50 : na, "بريكر دعم", bullColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 7, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and not cleanChartMode and bearishBreakerActive, bearishBreakerActive ? (bearishBreakerHigh + bearishBreakerLow) * 0.50 : na, "بريكر مقاومة", bearColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 8, stockChartAllowed and showWyckoffRangeLines and not cleanChartMode and wyckoffRangeReady, wyckoffRangeSupport, "دعم وايكوف", bullColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 9, stockChartAllowed and showWyckoffRangeLines and not cleanChartMode and wyckoffRangeReady, wyckoffRangeResistance, "مقاومة وايكوف", bearColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 10, candleLevelVisualAllowed and not na(highVolumeResistanceHigh) and not na(highVolumeResistanceLow), not na(highVolumeResistanceHigh) and not na(highVolumeResistanceLow) ? (highVolumeResistanceHigh + highVolumeResistanceLow) * 0.50 : na, "مقاومة حجم مرتفع", bearColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 11, candleLevelVisualAllowed and not na(highVolumeSupportHigh) and not na(highVolumeSupportLow), not na(highVolumeSupportHigh) and not na(highVolumeSupportLow) ? (highVolumeSupportHigh + highVolumeSupportLow) * 0.50 : na, "دعم حجم مرتفع", bullColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 12, candleLevelVisualAllowed and not na(lastKeyCandleHigh), lastKeyCandleHigh, "قمة شمعة مهمة", bearColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 13, candleLevelVisualAllowed and not na(lastKeyCandleLow), lastKeyCandleLow, "قاع شمعة مهمة", bullColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 14, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and demandZoneVisualRelevant, demandZoneVisualRelevant ? (demandZoneHigh + demandZoneLow) * 0.50 : na, "منطقة طلب", bullColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 15, stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones and supplyZoneVisualRelevant, supplyZoneVisualRelevant ? (supplyZoneHigh + supplyZoneLow) * 0.50 : na, "منطقة عرض", bearColor, safeAtr)

float fibStopMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
float fibTargetMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
float nearestFibSupportForStop = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibResistanceForStop = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibSupportForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibResistanceForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000, na, na, na, na)

// =============================================================================
// التوافق مقسم إلى مجموعات مستقلة قدر الإمكان، ويستخدم مقاما ديناميكيا حسب
// الأدوات المتاحة والمفعلة بدلا من عد الحدث السعري نفسه عدة مرات.
f_calculate_evidence_aggregates() =>
    bool swingStructureReady = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and swingLevelsCompatible
    int structureEvidenceMax = swingStructureReady ? 1 : 0
    int bullishStructureEvidence = swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : 0
    int bearishStructureEvidence = swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? 1 : 0

    // الحجم وVSA مجموعة مشاركة واحدة لأنهما يقرآن جهد الشمعة نفسه.
    bool bullDirectVolumeEvidence = useVolumeFilter and longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.directBreak and volumeAvailable
    bool bearDirectVolumeEvidence = useVolumeFilter and shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.directBreak and volumeAvailable
    bool vsaEvidenceEnabled = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable
    int participationEvidenceMax = bullDirectVolumeEvidence or bearDirectVolumeEvidence or vsaEvidenceEnabled or volumeAvailable ? 1 : 0
    int bullishParticipationEvidence = (bullDirectVolumeEvidence and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation >= 0.55) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == 1) or (volumeAvailable and obvSignal == 1) ? 1 : 0
    int bearishParticipationEvidence = (bearDirectVolumeEvidence and standardVolumeEffort and closeLocation <= 0.45) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == -1) or (volumeAvailable and obvSignal == -1) ? 1 : 0

    float evidenceVwapValue = useVwapFilter ? entryVwapValue : useStructuralVwap and confirmedStructuralVwapReady ? entryVwapValue : na
    bool vwapEvidenceReady = not na(evidenceVwapValue)
    bool zoneEvidenceReady = demandZoneEvidenceRelevant or supplyZoneEvidenceRelevant or bullishBreakerEvidenceRelevant or bearishBreakerEvidenceRelevant
    bool horizontalSrEvidenceReady = horizontalSrSignal != 0
    int rawLocationEvidenceMax = (majorTrendReady ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0)
    int locationEvidenceMax = math.min(3, rawLocationEvidenceMax)
    int bullishLocationRaw = (majorTrendReady and majorChannelSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close > evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishLocationRaw = (majorTrendReady and majorChannelSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close < evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bullishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, bullishLocationRaw)
    int bearishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, bearishLocationRaw)

    // Seven post-decline/post-advance confirmations are compressed into one vote:
    // swing sequence, level break, rising participation, EMA20/50, RSI50, MACD, OBV.
    int bullishRecoveryConfirmations = (lastLowTag == "HL" ? 1 : 0) + (bullishBreak or strongResistanceBreak ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= 1.0 and relativeVolume > nz(relativeVolume[1], relativeVolume) and close > open ? 1 : 0) + (movingAverageBias == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (rsiValue > 50 ? 1 : 0) + (macdLine > macdSignalLine and macdLine > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (obvSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishRecoveryConfirmations = (lastHighTag == "LH" ? 1 : 0) + (bearishBreak or strongSupportBreak ? 1 : 0) + (volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= 1.0 and relativeVolume > nz(relativeVolume[1], relativeVolume) and close < open ? 1 : 0) + (movingAverageBias == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (rsiValue < 50 ? 1 : 0) + (macdLine < macdSignalLine and macdLine < 0 ? 1 : 0) + (obvSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0)
    bool bullishRecoveryCluster = precedingDownContext and bullishRecoveryConfirmations >= 4
    bool bearishRecoveryCluster = precedingUpContext and bearishRecoveryConfirmations >= 4

    int barsSinceBullishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bullishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline)
    int barsSinceBearishPriceAction = ta.barssince(bearishInsideBarBreakout or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance)
    bool recentBullishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBullishPriceAction) and barsSinceBullishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    bool recentBearishPriceAction = not na(barsSinceBearishPriceAction) and barsSinceBearishPriceAction <= contextLookback
    // المرشح النشط هو زناد الخطة الأساسي. الأدلة السلوكية الأخرى تؤكد جودته
    // ولا تجعل BOS الصالح يبدأ من صفر في مجموعة الزناد.
    int triggerEvidenceMax = math.min(2, 1 + (useCandleConfirmation ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence ? 1 : 0))
    bool longOriginAuction = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION
    bool shortOriginAuction = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION
    bool longOriginCandle = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE
    bool shortOriginCandle = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE
    bool longOriginPriceAction = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN
    bool shortOriginPriceAction = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN
    bool longOriginDivergence = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_DIVERGENCE
    bool shortOriginDivergence = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.sourceKind == SETUP_SOURCE_DIVERGENCE
    int bullishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (longCandidateActive ? 1 : 0) + (recentBullishAuctionTrigger and not longOriginAuction ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBullishCandle and not longOriginCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBullishPriceAction and not longOriginPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBullishDivergence and not longOriginDivergence ? 1 : 0) + (bullishRecoveryCluster ? 1 : 0))
    int bearishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (shortCandidateActive ? 1 : 0) + (recentBearishAuctionTrigger and not shortOriginAuction ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and recentBearishCandle and not shortOriginCandle ? 1 : 0) + (usePriceActionBreakouts and recentBearishPriceAction and not shortOriginPriceAction ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBearishDivergence and not shortOriginDivergence ? 1 : 0) + (bearishRecoveryCluster ? 1 : 0))

    int evidenceMaximum = math.max(1, structureEvidenceMax + participationEvidenceMax + locationEvidenceMax + triggerEvidenceMax)
    int bullishEvidence = bullishStructureEvidence + bullishParticipationEvidence + bullishLocationEvidence + bullishTriggerEvidence
    int bearishEvidence = bearishStructureEvidence + bearishParticipationEvidence + bearishLocationEvidence + bearishTriggerEvidence
    int bullishEvidenceAxes = (bullishStructureEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bullishParticipationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bullishLocationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bullishTriggerEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    int bearishEvidenceAxes = (bearishStructureEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bearishParticipationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bearishLocationEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (bearishTriggerEvidence > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    [evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, 100.0 * float(bullishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum), 100.0 * float(bearishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum), bullishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidenceAxes]

[evidenceMaximum, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent, bullishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidenceAxes] = f_calculate_evidence_aggregates()
var int closedStructureBias = 0
var bool closedStructureConflict = false
var string closedWyckoffPhaseState = "لا تسلسل وايكوف"
var string closedZoneState = "لا منطقة"
var int closedHigherTrendBias = 0
var bool closedVolumeAvailable = false
var float closedRelativeVolume = na

if barstate.isconfirmed
    closedStructureBias := resolvedStructureBias
    closedStructureConflict := structureDirectionConflict
    closedWyckoffPhaseState := wyckoffPhaseState
    closedZoneState := zoneState
    closedHigherTrendBias := higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 1 : higherWeightedScore < 0 ? -1 : 0
    closedVolumeAvailable := volumeAvailable
    closedRelativeVolume := relativeVolume

// حالة الخطة ثابتة بين المباشر وBar Replay. الاختراق والوقف الفني لا يثبتان إلا بالإغلاق،
// بينما تبقى الأهداف السعرية محسوبة من مدى الشمعة.
var int planDirection = 0
var bool planActive = false
var int planStartBar = na
var float planEntry = na
var float planStop = na
var float planTarget1 = na
var float planTarget2 = na
var float planTarget3 = na
var int planTargetCount = 3
var int planScore = 0
var int planScoreMax = 1
var bool target1Done = false
var bool target2Done = false
var bool target3Done = false
var bool planTrailingStopActive = false
var int planTrailingStopStartedBar = na
var int target1HitBar = na
var int target2HitBar = na
var int target3HitBar = na
var float planHighSinceEntry = na
var float planLowSinceEntry = na
var bool planCounterTrend = false
var bool planHigherRisk = false
var string planSummary = ""
var int lastPlanExitBar = na
var int planOutcomeBar = na
var int planOutcomeKind = 0
var float planOutcomePrice = na

var line entryLine = na
var line stopLine = na
var line target1Line = na
var line target2Line = na
var line target3Line = na
var label entryLevelLabel = na
var label stopLevelLabel = na
var label target1LevelLabel = na
var label target2LevelLabel = na
var label target3LevelLabel = na
var label activePlanEntryMarker = na
var label activePlanTarget1Marker = na
var label activePlanTarget2Marker = na
var label activePlanTarget3Marker = na
var label activePlanOutcomeMarker = na
// قيّم الخطة القائمة أولا. بهذه الأولوية لا تستطيع إشارة جديدة أن تمسح وقفا
// أو هدفا حدث على الشمعة نفسها. الأهداف السعرية تُسجل أولا، والوقف الفني لا يعتمد إلا بإغلاق الشمعة.
if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    planHighSinceEntry := na(planHighSinceEntry) ? high : math.max(planHighSinceEntry, high)
    planLowSinceEntry := na(planLowSinceEntry) ? low : math.min(planLowSinceEntry, low)

bool planStopTouchedNow = planActive and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? low <= planStop : high >= planStop)
bool planStopClosedNow = planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? close <= planStop : close >= planStop)
bool planStopTriggeredNow = stopUsesTouch ? planStopTouchedNow : planStopClosedNow

if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    bool rawTarget1Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget1 : low <= planTarget1
    bool rawTarget2Touched = planTargetCount >= 2 and (planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget2 : low <= planTarget2)
    bool rawTarget3Touched = planTargetCount >= 3 and (planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget3 : low <= planTarget3)
    bool newTarget1Touched = rawTarget1Touched and not target1Done
    bool newTarget2Touched = rawTarget2Touched and not target2Done
    bool newTarget3Touched = rawTarget3Touched and not target3Done
    bool anyNewTargetTouched = newTarget1Touched or newTarget2Touched or newTarget3Touched
    bool touchStopHasPriority = stopUsesTouch and planStopTouchedNow
    bool closeStopAmbiguous = not stopUsesTouch and planStopClosedNow and anyNewTargetTouched
    bool intrabarOutcomeAmbiguous = closeStopAmbiguous
    bool target1Touched = not touchStopHasPriority and not intrabarOutcomeAmbiguous and newTarget1Touched
    bool target2Touched = not touchStopHasPriority and not intrabarOutcomeAmbiguous and newTarget2Touched
    bool target3Touched = not touchStopHasPriority and not intrabarOutcomeAmbiguous and newTarget3Touched

    if intrabarOutcomeAmbiguous
        planActive := false
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 5
        planOutcomePrice := close
        f_close_plan_bundle(entryLine, stopLine, target1Line, target2Line, target3Line, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 78, 78, 84)
    else if target3Touched and not target3Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        if not target2Done
            target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        target3Done := true
        target3HitBar := bar_index
        planActive := false
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 3
        planOutcomePrice := planTarget3
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
        target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
        target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
    else
        if target2Touched and not target2Done
            if not target1Done
                target1HitBar := bar_index
            target2HitBar := bar_index
            target1Done := true
            target2Done := true
            target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
            target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
        else if target1Touched and not target1Done
            target1Done := true
            target1HitBar := bar_index
            if planTargetCount == 1
                planActive := false
                lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
                planOutcomeBar := bar_index
                planOutcomeKind := 3
                planOutcomePrice := planTarget1
                f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
                f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
                target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
                target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
                target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
            else
                target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)

        if planActive and planStopTriggeredNow
            planActive := false
            lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
            planOutcomeBar := bar_index
            planOutcomeKind := 1
            planOutcomePrice := stopUsesTouch ? planStop : close
            f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
            f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 12, 2)
            if not target1Done
                f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
            if not target2Done
                f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
            if not target3Done
                f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)

// Only after the confirmed close do we calculate the next bar's protected
// stop. This prevents the current high from raising a stop that is then tested
// against the current low when their true intrabar order is unknown.
if planActive and usePostTargetAtrTrail and target1Done and barstate.isconfirmed and postTargetTrailReady
    float rawTrailCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? planHighSinceEntry - postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple : planLowSinceEntry + postTargetTrailAtr * postTargetTrailMultiple
    float breakEvenProtectedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.max(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry) : math.min(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry)
    float priceValidCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.min(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float tickRoundedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? f_floor_to_tick(priceValidCandidate) : f_ceil_to_tick(priceValidCandidate)
    bool candidateProtectsEntry = planDirection == 1 ? tickRoundedCandidate >= planEntry : tickRoundedCandidate <= planEntry
    bool candidateImprovesStop = planDirection == 1 ? tickRoundedCandidate > planStop + syminfo.mintick : tickRoundedCandidate < planStop - syminfo.mintick
    if candidateProtectsEntry and candidateImprovesStop
        planStop := tickRoundedCandidate
        planTrailingStopActive := true
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na(planTrailingStopStartedBar) ? bar_index : planTrailingStopStartedBar
        if not na(stopLine)
            line.set_xy1(stopLine, bar_index, planStop)
            line.set_xy2(stopLine, bar_index + 1, planStop)
            line.set_extend(stopLine, extend.right)
            line.set_color(stopLine, color.new(#F23645, planStopLineTransparency))
            line.set_style(stopLine, line.style_dotted)
            line.set_width(stopLine, 1)

int earlyRetestMaximumAge = chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 2 : 1
int longCandidateAge = longCandidateActive and not na(longCandidateState.eventBar) ? bar_index - longCandidateState.eventBar : 100000
int shortCandidateAge = shortCandidateActive and not na(shortCandidateState.eventBar) ? bar_index - shortCandidateState.eventBar : 100000
bool freshLongRetest = allowEarlyQualifiedRetest and longCandidateActive and not longCandidateState.directBreak and longCandidateState.transitionEligible and longCandidateAge <= earlyRetestMaximumAge
bool freshShortRetest = allowEarlyQualifiedRetest and shortCandidateActive and not shortCandidateState.directBreak and shortCandidateState.transitionEligible and shortCandidateAge <= earlyRetestMaximumAge
bool bullishEarlyTriggerNow = freshLongRetest and (bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bullishBreakerRetest or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or bullishRetest or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed or bullishSweep or bullishImportantCandleConfirmation or bullishRsiDivergence or highVolumeBottomZoneEvent)
bool bearishEarlyTriggerNow = freshShortRetest and (bearishWyckoffTest or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishBreakerRetest or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or bearishRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed or bearishSweep or bearishImportantCandleConfirmation or bearishRsiDivergence or highVolumePeakZoneEvent)
bool bullishEarlyCloseQuality = bullishEarlyTriggerNow and close > open and closeLocation >= 0.55 and vsaSignal != -1
bool bearishEarlyCloseQuality = bearishEarlyTriggerNow and close < open and closeLocation <= 0.45 and vsaSignal != 1
bool longEarlyOpportunityCandidate = enableEarlyOpportunityEngine and longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY
bool shortEarlyOpportunityCandidate = enableEarlyOpportunityEngine and shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY
float longEvidenceDiscount = math.min(10.0, (freshLongRetest and bullishEarlyCloseQuality ? 5.0 : 0.0) + (longEarlyOpportunityCandidate ? earlyOpportunityEvidenceDiscount : 0.0))
float shortEvidenceDiscount = math.min(10.0, (freshShortRetest and bearishEarlyCloseQuality ? 5.0 : 0.0) + (shortEarlyOpportunityCandidate ? earlyOpportunityEvidenceDiscount : 0.0))
float longNormalEvidenceMinimum = math.min(90.0, math.max(45.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - longEvidenceDiscount))
float shortNormalEvidenceMinimum = math.min(90.0, math.max(45.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - shortEvidenceDiscount))
float directEvidenceMinimum = math.min(90.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 7.5)
bool exceptionalLongBreakPass = f_exceptional_direct_break_pass(true, longCandidateActive, longCandidateState.directBreak, longCandidateState.qualified, volumePass, bodyRatio, closeLocation, bullishEvidenceAxes, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, higherWeightedScore, longCandidateState.reversalEligible)
bool exceptionalShortBreakPass = f_exceptional_direct_break_pass(false, shortCandidateActive, shortCandidateState.directBreak, shortCandidateState.qualified, volumePass, bodyRatio, closeLocation, bearishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidence, higherWeightedScore, shortCandidateState.reversalEligible)
bool longEvidencePass = f_entry_evidence_pass(true, useAdaptiveEntryQuality, longCandidateState.directBreak, bullishEarlyCloseQuality, longNormalEvidenceMinimum, directEvidenceMinimum, longNormalEvidenceMinimum, higherLongPenalty, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent) or exceptionalLongBreakPass
bool shortEvidencePass = f_entry_evidence_pass(false, useAdaptiveEntryQuality, shortCandidateState.directBreak, bearishEarlyCloseQuality, shortNormalEvidenceMinimum, directEvidenceMinimum, shortNormalEvidenceMinimum, higherShortPenalty, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent) or exceptionalShortBreakPass
bool longConsensusPass = f_independent_consensus_pass(longCandidateState.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY, bullishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidenceAxes, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidencePercent, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidence, math.max(75.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 15.0)) or exceptionalLongBreakPass
bool shortConsensusPass = f_independent_consensus_pass(shortCandidateState.eventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY, bearishEvidenceAxes, bullishEvidenceAxes, bearishEvidencePercent, bullishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidence, bullishEvidence, math.max(75.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 15.0)) or exceptionalShortBreakPass
// الاتجاهات ليست أصواتا مكررة فقط؛ هي بوابة سلامة. يمنع التعارض البنيوي
// أو معارضة القناة الرئيسية الخطة العادية. الاستثناء الوحيد هو انعكاس مؤكد غيّر
// الهيكل وامتلك فارق أدلة واضحا مع كسر/اختبار فني معتبر.
// Decision engine is isolated to keep the compiled main body below TradingView limits.
f_trade_decision_context() =>
    float counterTrendEvidenceMinimum = math.max(65.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 10.0)
    bool bullishReversalTrigger = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.reversalEligible
    bool bearishReversalTrigger = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.reversalEligible
    bool bullishConfirmedReversalPass = bullishReversalTrigger and (resolvedStructureBias == 1 or longCandidateState.directBreak and movingAverageBias != -1 and close > ema20) and bullishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
    bool bearishConfirmedReversalPass = bearishReversalTrigger and (resolvedStructureBias == -1 or shortCandidateState.directBreak and movingAverageBias != 1 and close < ema20) and bearishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
    bool analysisIntegrityPass = swingLevelsCompatible
    bool majorLongOpposed = majorTrendReady and majorChannelSignal == -1
    bool majorShortOpposed = majorTrendReady and majorChannelSignal == 1
    bool longMacroContinuationPass = longCandidateState.stopKind % 10 != 1 or not macroTrendReady or macroChannelBias != -1
    bool shortMacroContinuationPass = shortCandidateState.stopKind % 10 != 1 or not macroTrendReady or macroChannelBias != 1
    float transitionEvidenceMinimum = math.max(60.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 5.0)
    bool bullishTransitionPass = longCandidateActive and longCandidateState.transitionEligible and movingAverageBias != -1 and close > ema20 and bullishEvidencePercent >= transitionEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
    bool bearishTransitionPass = shortCandidateActive and shortCandidateState.transitionEligible and movingAverageBias != 1 and close < ema20 and bearishEvidencePercent >= transitionEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
    float earlyReversalEvidenceMinimum = math.min(90.0, math.max(47.5, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - earlyOpportunityEvidenceDiscount))
    bool bullishEarlyOpportunityPass = longEarlyOpportunityCandidate and bullishEarlyCloseQuality and bullishEvidencePercent >= earlyReversalEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 1 and higherWeightedScore >= 0
    bool bearishEarlyOpportunityPass = shortEarlyOpportunityCandidate and bearishEarlyCloseQuality and bearishEvidencePercent >= earlyReversalEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 1 and higherWeightedScore <= 0
    bool bullishEarlyContextPass = bullishEarlyOpportunityPass and not majorLongOpposed and not structureDirectionConflict and resolvedStructureBias != -1
    bool bearishEarlyContextPass = bearishEarlyOpportunityPass and not majorShortOpposed and not structureDirectionConflict and resolvedStructureBias != 1
    int macdContextBias = macdLine > macdSignalLine and macdLine > 0 ? 1 : macdLine < macdSignalLine and macdLine < 0 ? -1 : 0
    int channelContextBias = majorChannelSignal
    int volumeContextBias = obvSignal != 0 ? obvSignal : volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= 1.0 ? (close > open ? 1 : close < open ? -1 : 0) : 0
    int fibContextBias = (longCandidateActive and not longCandidateState.directBreak) or (shortCandidateActive and not shortCandidateState.directBreak) ? dealingRangeSignal : 0
    int higherContextBias = higherWeightedScore > 0 ? 1 : higherWeightedScore < 0 ? -1 : 0
    int technicalContextScore = resolvedStructureBias * 4 + horizontalSrSignal * 4 + channelContextBias * 3 + higherContextBias * 3 + movingAverageBias * 2 + wyckoffContextSignal * 2 + macdContextBias + volumeContextBias + fibContextBias
    int technicalContextBias = technicalContextScore >= 5 ? 1 : technicalContextScore <= -5 ? -1 : 0
    float adxStrongLevel = math.max(20.0, math.min(28.0, nz(ta.sma(adxValue, 50), 23.0) + 2.0))
    bool adxReady = not na(adxValue) and not na(plusDi) and not na(minusDi)
    bool adxStrong = adxReady and adxValue >= adxStrongLevel and (adxValue >= nz(adxValue[1], adxValue) or adxValue >= adxStrongLevel + 5.0)
    bool immediateLongDmiBlock = adxReady and adxValue >= adxStrongLevel and minusDi >= plusDi + 5.0
    bool immediateShortDmiBlock = adxReady and adxValue >= adxStrongLevel and plusDi >= minusDi + 5.0
    int marketRegimeNow = not adxReady ? 3 : sidewaysRangeReady and adxValue < adxStrongLevel - 5.0 and movingAverageBias == 0 and channelContextBias == 0 ? 0 : adxStrong and technicalContextBias == 1 and resolvedStructureBias != -1 and plusDi > minusDi and obvSignal != -1 ? 1 : adxStrong and technicalContextBias == -1 and resolvedStructureBias != 1 and minusDi > plusDi and obvSignal != 1 ? -1 : 2
    var int marketRegimeState = 2
    marketRegimeState := barstate.isconfirmed and marketRegimeNow == marketRegimeNow[1] ? marketRegimeNow : marketRegimeState
    bool bullishLimitedContextPass = (bullishTransitionPass or bullishEarlyContextPass) and not majorLongOpposed and resolvedStructureBias != -1 and not structureDirectionConflict and higherWeightedScore >= 0
    bool bearishLimitedContextPass = (bearishTransitionPass or bearishEarlyContextPass) and not majorShortOpposed and resolvedStructureBias != 1 and not structureDirectionConflict and higherWeightedScore <= 0
    bool longRegimePass = not immediateLongDmiBlock and (marketRegimeState != -1 or bullishConfirmedReversalPass)
    bool shortRegimePass = not immediateShortDmiBlock and (marketRegimeState != 1 or bearishConfirmedReversalPass)
    bool longTrendLayersPass = ((not majorLongOpposed and not structureDirectionConflict) and technicalContextBias != -1) or bullishConfirmedReversalPass or bullishLimitedContextPass
    bool shortTrendLayersPass = ((not majorShortOpposed and not structureDirectionConflict) and technicalContextBias != 1) or bearishConfirmedReversalPass or bearishLimitedContextPass
    bool higherLongAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherTimeframesReady and (higherWeightedScore > -5 or bullishConfirmedReversalPass or bullishLimitedContextPass)
    bool higherShortAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherTimeframesReady and (higherWeightedScore < 5 or bearishConfirmedReversalPass or bearishLimitedContextPass)
    bool primaryLongSrPass = horizontalSrSignal != -1 or longCandidateState.usesGuard and not na(longCandidateState.guardLevel) and close >= longCandidateState.guardLevel - retestTolerance
    bool primaryShortSrPass = horizontalSrSignal != 1 or shortCandidateState.usesGuard and not na(shortCandidateState.guardLevel) and close <= shortCandidateState.guardLevel + retestTolerance
    bool longPlanReady = actionableLongSetup and primaryLongSrPass and analysisIntegrityPass and evidenceMaximum >= 3 and longMacroContinuationPass and longRegimePass and longTrendLayersPass and longEvidencePass and longConsensusPass and higherLongAlignmentPass
    bool shortPlanReady = actionableShortSetup and primaryShortSrPass and analysisIntegrityPass and evidenceMaximum >= 3 and shortMacroContinuationPass and shortRegimePass and shortTrendLayersPass and shortEvidencePass and shortConsensusPass and higherShortAlignmentPass
    bool longDecisionEdge = bullishEvidencePercent >= bearishEvidencePercent + 7.5 and resolvedStructureBias != -1 and higherWeightedScore >= 0 and technicalContextBias != -1
    bool shortDecisionEdge = bearishEvidencePercent >= bullishEvidencePercent + 7.5 and resolvedStructureBias != 1 and higherWeightedScore <= 0 and technicalContextBias != 1
    int standardDirection = longPlanReady and shortPlanReady ? longDecisionEdge ? 1 : shortDecisionEdge ? -1 : 0 : longPlanReady ? 1 : shortPlanReady ? -1 : 0
    // فرصة فنية جيدة لكن أعلى مخاطرة: لا تتجاوز سلامة الوقف أو العائق
    // البنيوي، وتحتاج محورين مستقلين من الأدلة مع غلبة واضحة للاتجاه.
    float higherRiskEvidenceMinimum = math.max(42.5, math.min(55.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent - 10.0))
    bool longHigherRiskReady = standardDirection == 0 and actionableLongSetup and primaryLongSrPass and analysisIntegrityPass and evidenceMaximum >= 3 and bullishEvidenceAxes >= 2 and bullishEvidencePercent >= higherRiskEvidenceMinimum + higherLongPenalty * 0.50 and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 1 and not structureDirectionConflict and not immediateLongDmiBlock and technicalContextBias != -1 and (not majorLongOpposed or longCandidateState.reversalEligible) and (longMacroContinuationPass or longCandidateState.reversalEligible) and (not higherTimeframesReady or higherWeightedScore > -8 or longCandidateState.reversalEligible)
    bool shortHigherRiskReady = standardDirection == 0 and actionableShortSetup and primaryShortSrPass and analysisIntegrityPass and evidenceMaximum >= 3 and bearishEvidenceAxes >= 2 and bearishEvidencePercent >= higherRiskEvidenceMinimum + higherShortPenalty * 0.50 and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 1 and not structureDirectionConflict and not immediateShortDmiBlock and technicalContextBias != 1 and (not majorShortOpposed or shortCandidateState.reversalEligible) and (shortMacroContinuationPass or shortCandidateState.reversalEligible) and (not higherTimeframesReady or higherWeightedScore < 8 or shortCandidateState.reversalEligible)
    bool longHigherRiskEdge = bullishEvidencePercent >= bearishEvidencePercent + 5.0 and (resolvedStructureBias != -1 or longCandidateState.reversalEligible)
    bool shortHigherRiskEdge = bearishEvidencePercent >= bullishEvidencePercent + 5.0 and (resolvedStructureBias != 1 or shortCandidateState.reversalEligible)
    int higherRiskDirection = longHigherRiskReady and shortHigherRiskReady ? longHigherRiskEdge ? 1 : shortHigherRiskEdge ? -1 : 0 : longHigherRiskReady ? 1 : shortHigherRiskReady ? -1 : 0
    int requestedPlanDirection = standardDirection != 0 ? standardDirection : higherRiskDirection
    bool requestedPlanHigherRisk = standardDirection == 0 and higherRiskDirection != 0
    [requestedPlanDirection, technicalContextBias, requestedPlanHigherRisk]
[requestedPlanDirection, technicalContextBias, requestedPlanHigherRisk] = f_trade_decision_context()

// جميع نهايات الخطة تمر من هنا: أوقف الخطوط، احذف تفاصيل الخطة، واحتفظ
// بعلامة نتيجة واحدة فقط. تتكرر الكتلة أثناء الشمعة الحية حتى تثبت الرسوم بعد الإغلاق.
bool planEndedThisBar = not planActive and not na(planOutcomeBar) and planOutcomeBar == bar_index
if planEndedThisBar
    entryLine := f_clear_line(entryLine)
    stopLine := f_clear_line(stopLine)
    target1Line := f_clear_line(target1Line)
    target2Line := f_clear_line(target2Line)
    target3Line := f_clear_line(target3Line)
    entryLevelLabel := f_clear_label(entryLevelLabel)
    stopLevelLabel := f_clear_label(stopLevelLabel)
    target1LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target1LevelLabel)
    target2LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target2LevelLabel)
    target3LevelLabel := f_clear_label(target3LevelLabel)
    activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
    activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
    activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
    activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
    planHighSinceEntry := na
    planLowSinceEntry := na
bool planCooldownComplete = na(lastPlanExitBar) or bar_index - lastPlanExitBar > planReentryCooldownBars
bool createNewPlan = barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and planCooldownComplete
bool newPlanCreatedEvent = false

if createNewPlan
    bool isLong = requestedPlanDirection == 1
    SetupCandidateState planCandidateState = isLong ? longCandidateState : shortCandidateState
    float setupReferenceLevel = planCandidateState.ref
    float genericSwingRange = f_setup_wave_range(isLong, planCandidateState.eventBar, math.max(retestWindow * 4, pivotBars * 12), lastSwingHigh, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLow, lastSwingLowBar)
    float setupRawRange = planCandidateState.rng
    float baseContextualStopAnchor = planCandidateState.stop
    int candidateStopMode = int(math.floor(float(planCandidateState.stopKind) / 10.0))
    bool protectedContextStop = candidateStopMode == 2
    float setupSpecificStopAnchor = candidateStopMode == 1 ? planCandidateState.stop : na
    float nearestFibStopLevel = isLong ? nearestFibSupportForStop : nearestFibResistanceForStop
    float majorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : majorChannelBias == 1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na) : (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelBias == -1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na)
    float stopCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, isLong ? highVolumeSupportLow : highVolumeResistanceHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleLow : lastKeyCandleHigh, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float protectedSwingStop = isLong ? lastSwingLow : lastSwingHigh
    float stopClusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.30, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    [minimumStopAtrMultiple, maximumStopAtrMultiple] = f_stop_atr_limits(chartSeconds)
    float stopMaximumDistance = safeAtr * (maximumStopAtrMultiple + stopAtrBuffer)
    float primaryClusterStop = isLong ? f_nearest_price_level(false, close, safeAtr * 0.10, strongSupportReady and not supportClusterActsAsResistance ? strongSupportLow : na, strongResistanceRoleReversed and close >= strongResistanceLow - breakBuffer ? strongResistanceLow : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : f_nearest_price_level(true, close, safeAtr * 0.10, strongResistanceReady and not resistanceClusterActsAsSupport ? strongResistanceHigh : na, strongSupportRoleReversed and close <= strongSupportHigh + breakBuffer ? strongSupportHigh : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float historicalPivotStop = f_nearest_stored_pivot(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, stopMaximumDistance, isLong ? srLowPivotPrices : srHighPivotPrices, isLong ? srLowPivotBars : srHighPivotBars, isLong ? srLowPivotTimes : srHighPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    float primaryPivotStop = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, protectedSwingStop, historicalPivotStop, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    array<float> stopClusterLevels = array.from(primaryClusterStop, primaryPivotStop, isLong ? higherTimeframeSupport : higherTimeframeResistance, setupSpecificStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor, isLong ? demandZoneLow : supplyZoneHigh, isLong ? wyckoffRangeSupport : wyckoffRangeResistance, majorChannelStopLevel, nearestFibStopLevel, stopCandleLevel)
    [rankedStopAnchor, _, _, _] = f_ranked_cluster_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, stopMaximumDistance, stopClusterTolerance, stopClusterLevels)
    float hierarchyStopAnchor = not na(rankedStopAnchor) ? rankedStopAnchor : not na(setupSpecificStopAnchor) ? setupSpecificStopAnchor : baseContextualStopAnchor
    float contextualStopAnchor = protectedContextStop and not na(baseContextualStopAnchor) and not na(hierarchyStopAnchor) ? (isLong ? math.min(hierarchyStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor) : math.max(hierarchyStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor)) : hierarchyStopAnchor
    bool contextualAnchorValid = not na(contextualStopAnchor) and (isLong ? contextualStopAnchor < close - syminfo.mintick : contextualStopAnchor > close + syminfo.mintick)
    bool rankedAnchorValid = not na(rankedStopAnchor) and (isLong ? rankedStopAnchor < close - syminfo.mintick : rankedStopAnchor > close + syminfo.mintick)
    float validInvalidationAnchor = contextualAnchorValid ? contextualStopAnchor : rankedAnchorValid ? rankedStopAnchor : na
    float technicalInvalidationPrice = isLong ? validInvalidationAnchor - (stopUsesTouch ? safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer : 0.0) : validInvalidationAnchor + (stopUsesTouch ? safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer : 0.0)
    float currentPriceTick = f_market_tick(close)
    float minimumStopDistance = safeAtr * minimumStopAtrMultiple
    float validStopUnrounded = isLong ? math.min(technicalInvalidationPrice, close - math.max(currentPriceTick, minimumStopDistance)) : math.max(technicalInvalidationPrice, close + math.max(currentPriceTick, minimumStopDistance))
    float validStop = isLong ? f_floor_to_tick(validStopUnrounded) : f_ceil_to_tick(validStopUnrounded)
    float technicalRisk = math.max(math.abs(close - validStop), currentPriceTick)
    float confirmedWaveRange = not na(setupRawRange) and setupRawRange > currentPriceTick ? setupRawRange : genericSwingRange
    float waveRiskFloor = not na(confirmedWaveRange) ? math.min(safeAtr * maximumStopAtrMultiple, confirmedWaveRange * 0.15) : 0.0
    float targetSizingRisk = useAdaptiveRiskGeometry ? math.max(technicalRisk, math.max(safeAtr * minimumStopAtrMultiple, waveRiskFloor)) : technicalRisk
    float risk = technicalRisk
    float swingRange = not na(confirmedWaveRange) ? math.max(confirmedWaveRange, targetSizingRisk) : targetSizingRisk * 2.0

    // Risk already scales with the chart's structure and ATR. Do not shrink
    // reward below 1R on fast charts. Larger intervals may widen the ladder.
    float rewardHorizonFactor = math.max(targetHorizonFactor, 1.0)
    // The timeframe factor expands fallback R/Fibonacci objectives only.
    // Real structural levels keep their natural price and use the unscaled R ladder.
    float structuralRr1 = math.max(rr1, minimumTarget1RiskMultiple)
    float orderedRr1 = math.max(rr1 * rewardHorizonFactor, structuralRr1)
    float orderedRr2 = math.max(rr2 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr1 + 0.75)
    float orderedRr3 = math.max(rr3 * rewardHorizonFactor, orderedRr2 + 1.00)
    float minimumTarget1Distance = targetSizingRisk * orderedRr1
    float minimumTarget2Distance = targetSizingRisk * orderedRr2
    float minimumTarget3Distance = targetSizingRisk * orderedRr3
    float minimumTargetGap = targetSizingRisk * 0.50
    // الغلاف الأقصى لا يجوز أن يصبح أصغر من الحد الأدنى الذي اختاره المستخدم.
    float maximumTarget1Distance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * 2.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget1Distance)
    float maximumTarget2Distance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * 4.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget2Distance + minimumTargetGap)
    float maximumTarget3Distance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * 6.0 * rewardHorizonFactor, minimumTarget3Distance + minimumTargetGap)
    float candidateTarget1 = na
    float candidateTarget2 = na
    float candidateTarget3 = na
    float fibObjective1 = na
    float fibObjective2 = na
    float fibObjective3 = na
    if targetMode == RR_MODE
        candidateTarget1 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget1Distance : close - minimumTarget1Distance
        candidateTarget2 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget2Distance : close - minimumTarget2Distance
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? close + minimumTarget3Distance : close - minimumTarget3Distance
    else
        float brokenLevel = nz(setupReferenceLevel, isLong ? nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : nz(lastSwingLow, close))
        [fibTarget1, fibTarget2, fibTarget3, selectedFibLevel1, selectedFibLevel2, selectedFibLevel3] = f_build_fib_extension_targets(isLong, brokenLevel, swingRange, close, minimumTarget1Distance, minimumTarget2Distance, minimumTarget3Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget2Distance, maximumTarget3Distance, minimumTargetGap)
        candidateTarget1 := fibTarget1
        candidateTarget2 := fibTarget2
        candidateTarget3 := fibTarget3
        fibObjective1 := selectedFibLevel1
        fibObjective2 := selectedFibLevel2
        fibObjective3 := selectedFibLevel3

    float measuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMeasuredTarget : bearishMeasuredTarget
    if not na(measuredTarget) and (isLong ? measuredTarget > close : measuredTarget < close)
        // A measured objective may extend T3, but never beyond the adaptive
        // maximum reward envelope for this chart interval.
        float boundedMeasuredTarget = isLong ? math.min(measuredTarget, close + maximumTarget3Distance) : math.max(measuredTarget, close - maximumTarget3Distance)
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? math.max(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget) : math.min(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget)

    // محرك أهداف متعدد الطبقات: محاور تاريخية وعوائق بنيوية، ثم القناة
    // الرئيسية المؤكدة، ثم الأهداف المقاسة، ثم فيبوناتشي والمتوسطات.
    float minimumStructuralTargetDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float retracementTargetLevel = isLong ? nearestFibResistanceForTarget : nearestFibSupportForTarget
    float majorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerNow
    float majorChannelMeasuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMajorChannelTarget : bearishMajorChannelTarget
    float horizontalObstacleLevel = isLong ? (strongResistanceReady and not strongResistanceRoleReversed ? strongResistanceLow : na) : (strongSupportReady and not strongSupportRoleReversed ? strongSupportHigh : na)
    float opposingZoneBoundary = isLong ? f_nearest_price_level(true, close, syminfo.mintick, supplyZoneLow, bearishBreakerLow, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na) : f_nearest_price_level(false, close, syminfo.mintick, demandZoneHigh, bullishBreakerHigh, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float targetWyckoffLevel = isLong ? wyckoffRangeResistance : wyckoffRangeSupport
    float targetCandleLevel = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, isLong ? highVolumeResistanceLow : highVolumeSupportHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleHigh : lastKeyCandleLow, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float historicalPivotObstacle = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, isLong ? srHighPivotTimes : srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    bool insideOpposingHardZone = (isLong ? supplyZoneActive and close >= supplyZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= supplyZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick : demandZoneActive and close >= demandZoneLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= demandZoneHigh + syminfo.mintick) or (isLong ? bearishBreakerActive and close >= bearishBreakerLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + syminfo.mintick : bullishBreakerActive and close >= bullishBreakerLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= bullishBreakerHigh + syminfo.mintick) or (isLong ? strongResistanceReady and not resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and close >= strongResistanceLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= strongResistanceHigh + syminfo.mintick : strongSupportReady and not supportClusterActsAsResistance and close >= strongSupportLow - syminfo.mintick and close <= strongSupportHigh + syminfo.mintick)
    float nearestHardObstacle = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, syminfo.mintick * 2.0, historicalPivotObstacle, isLong ? higherTimeframeResistance : higherTimeframeSupport, horizontalObstacleLevel, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, na, na, na, na)
    float hardObstacleDistanceSizingR = not na(nearestHardObstacle) ? math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) / targetSizingRisk : na
    float requiredObstacleRoomR = math.max(minimumObstacleRoomRiskMultiple, orderedRr1)
    float shortOpportunityRoomR = math.max(0.50, minimumShortOpportunityR)
    bool shortOpportunityAvailable = allowShortOpportunityPlan and not insideOpposingHardZone and not na(hardObstacleDistanceSizingR) and hardObstacleDistanceSizingR >= shortOpportunityRoomR and hardObstacleDistanceSizingR < requiredObstacleRoomR
    bool roomToObstacleValid = not useAdaptiveRiskGeometry or (not insideOpposingHardZone and (na(hardObstacleDistanceSizingR) or hardObstacleDistanceSizingR >= requiredObstacleRoomR or shortOpportunityAvailable))
    float clusterTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.30, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float effectiveMinimumTarget1Distance = targetSizingRisk * (shortOpportunityAvailable ? shortOpportunityRoomR : orderedRr1)
    array<float> targetClusterLevels = array.from(horizontalObstacleLevel, isLong ? higherTimeframeResistance : higherTimeframeSupport, historicalPivotObstacle, opposingZoneBoundary, targetWyckoffLevel, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelMeasuredTarget, fibObjective1, retracementTargetLevel, maFast, ema50, targetCandleLevel)
    [rankedTarget1, _, _, _] = f_ranked_target_level(isLong, close, effectiveMinimumTarget1Distance, maximumTarget1Distance, clusterTolerance, targetClusterLevels)
    bool hardObstacleMeetsTargetDistance = not na(nearestHardObstacle) and math.abs(nearestHardObstacle - close) + syminfo.mintick * 0.5 >= effectiveMinimumTarget1Distance
    bool hardObstacleIsCloserOrEqual = not na(nearestHardObstacle) and (isLong ? nearestHardObstacle <= nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1) + syminfo.mintick : nearestHardObstacle >= nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1) - syminfo.mintick)
    bool firstTargetUsesHardObstacle = hardObstacleMeetsTargetDistance and (shortOpportunityAvailable or hardObstacleIsCloserOrEqual)
    float firstTargetCandidate = firstTargetUsesHardObstacle ? nearestHardObstacle : nz(rankedTarget1, candidateTarget1)
    candidateTarget1 := firstTargetCandidate
    float target2MinimumDistance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * orderedRr2, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float historicalPivotTarget2 = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, target2MinimumDistance, maximumTarget2Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, isLong ? srHighPivotTimes : srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    array.set(targetClusterLevels, 2, historicalPivotTarget2)
    array.set(targetClusterLevels, 7, fibObjective2)
    [rankedTarget2, _, _, _] = f_ranked_target_level(isLong, close, target2MinimumDistance, maximumTarget2Distance, clusterTolerance, targetClusterLevels)
    float fallbackTarget2Distance = math.max(target2MinimumDistance, math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float fallbackTarget2 = isLong ? close + fallbackTarget2Distance : close - fallbackTarget2Distance
    candidateTarget2 := nz(rankedTarget2, fallbackTarget2)
    float target3MinimumDistance = math.max(targetSizingRisk * orderedRr3, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float historicalPivotTarget3 = f_nearest_stored_pivot(isLong, close, target3MinimumDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, isLong ? srHighPivotPrices : srLowPivotPrices, isLong ? srHighPivotBars : srLowPivotBars, isLong ? srHighPivotTimes : srLowPivotTimes, srMaximumAgeMilliseconds)
    array.set(targetClusterLevels, 2, historicalPivotTarget3)
    array.set(targetClusterLevels, 7, fibObjective3)
    [rankedTarget3, _, _, _] = f_ranked_target_level(isLong, close, target3MinimumDistance, maximumTarget3Distance, clusterTolerance, targetClusterLevels)
    float fallbackTarget3Distance = math.max(target3MinimumDistance, math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) + minimumTargetGap)
    float fallbackTarget3 = isLong ? close + fallbackTarget3Distance : close - fallbackTarget3Distance
    candidateTarget3 := nz(rankedTarget3, fallbackTarget3)
    candidateTarget1 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget1) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget1)
    candidateTarget2 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget2) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget2)
    candidateTarget3 := isLong ? f_ceil_to_tick(candidateTarget3) : f_floor_to_tick(candidateTarget3)
    float planRiskPercent = close > 0 ? risk / close * 100.0 : 1000.0
    float planRiskAtrMultiple = safeAtr > 0 ? risk / safeAtr : 1000.0
    float adaptiveRiskPercentLimit = chartSeconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 20.0 : chartSeconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 25.0 : chartSeconds <= 31 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 35.0 : 45.0
    float effectiveRiskPercentLimit = useAdaptiveRiskGeometry ? math.min(maxPlanRiskPercent, adaptiveRiskPercentLimit) : maxPlanRiskPercent
    bool riskPercentValid = planRiskPercent <= effectiveRiskPercentLimit
    bool riskAtrValid = not useAdaptiveRiskGeometry or planRiskAtrMultiple <= maximumStopAtrMultiple + 0.0001
    bool riskDistanceValid = riskPercentValid and riskAtrValid
    bool stopPriceValid = validStop >= syminfo.mintick and (isLong ? validStop < close : validStop > close)
    bool quickPlanNumbersReady = not na(close) and not na(validStop) and not na(risk) and not na(candidateTarget1)
    bool standardPlanNumbersReady = quickPlanNumbersReady and not na(candidateTarget2) and not na(candidateTarget3)
    bool planNumbersReady = shortOpportunityAvailable ? quickPlanNumbersReady : standardPlanNumbersReady
    bool targetPricesPositive = shortOpportunityAvailable ? quickPlanNumbersReady and candidateTarget1 >= syminfo.mintick : standardPlanNumbersReady and candidateTarget1 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget2 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget3 >= syminfo.mintick
    bool targetOrderValid = shortOpportunityAvailable ? quickPlanNumbersReady and (isLong ? candidateTarget1 > close : candidateTarget1 < close) : standardPlanNumbersReady and (isLong ? candidateTarget1 > close and candidateTarget2 > candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 > candidateTarget2 : candidateTarget1 < close and candidateTarget2 < candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 < candidateTarget2)
    float target1RiskMultiple = quickPlanNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget1 - close) / risk : na
    float target2RiskMultiple = standardPlanNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget2 - close) / risk : na
    float target3RiskMultiple = standardPlanNumbersReady ? math.abs(candidateTarget3 - close) / risk : na
    bool shortOpportunityTargetValid = quickPlanNumbersReady and shortOpportunityAvailable and firstTargetUsesHardObstacle and target1RiskMultiple + 0.0001 >= shortOpportunityRoomR
    bool standardTargetSequenceValid = standardPlanNumbersReady and target1RiskMultiple + 0.0001 >= orderedRr1 and target2RiskMultiple + 0.0001 >= orderedRr2 and target3RiskMultiple + 0.0001 >= orderedRr3 and target2RiskMultiple >= target1RiskMultiple + 0.49 and target3RiskMultiple >= target2RiskMultiple + 0.49
    bool targetRewardValid = shortOpportunityTargetValid or standardTargetSequenceValid
    bool planGeometryValid = planNumbersReady and riskDistanceValid and roomToObstacleValid and stopPriceValid and targetPricesPositive and targetOrderValid and targetRewardValid

    if planGeometryValid
        int score = isLong ? bullishEvidence : bearishEvidence
        planDirection := isLong ? 1 : -1
        planActive := true
        planStartBar := bar_index
        planEntry := close
        planStop := validStop
        planTarget1 := candidateTarget1
        planTarget2 := candidateTarget2
        planTarget3 := candidateTarget3
        planTargetCount := shortOpportunityTargetValid ? 1 : 3
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planScore := score
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := isLong ? higherWeightedScore < 0 : higherWeightedScore > 0
        planHigherRisk := requestedPlanHigherRisk
        planSummary := (requestedPlanHigherRisk ? (isLong ? "شراء فني - مخاطرة أعلى" : "بيع فني - مخاطرة أعلى") : (isLong ? "شراء" : "بيع")) + " | " + planCandidateState.source
        planOutcomeBar := na
        planOutcomeKind := 0
        planOutcomePrice := na
        newPlanCreatedEvent := true
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na
        f_clear_setup_candidate(planCandidateState)

        // هذه هي شمعة القرار الفعلية: لا تظهر العلامة إلا بعد إغلاق الشمعة
        // واكتمال الإعداد والأدلة وإدارة المخاطرة، لا لمجرد اقتراب السعر.

        activePlanEntryMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanEntryMarker)
        activePlanTarget1Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget1Marker)
        activePlanTarget2Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget2Marker)
        activePlanTarget3Marker := f_clear_label(activePlanTarget3Marker)
        activePlanOutcomeMarker := f_clear_label(activePlanOutcomeMarker)
        color confirmedEntryColor = isLong ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor
        entryLine := f_replace_plan_line(entryLine, true, planEntry, confirmedEntryColor, planEntryLineTransparency, line.style_solid)
        stopLine := f_replace_plan_line(stopLine, true, planStop, #F23645, planStopLineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target1Line := f_replace_plan_line(target1Line, true, planTarget1, #22C55E, planTarget1LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target2Line := f_replace_plan_line(target2Line, planTargetCount >= 2, planTarget2, #22C55E, planTarget2LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        target3Line := f_replace_plan_line(target3Line, planTargetCount >= 3, planTarget3, #22C55E, planTarget3LineTransparency, line.style_dotted)
        entryLevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(entryLevelLabel, showPlanLevelLabels, planEntry, confirmedEntryColor)
        stopLevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(stopLevelLabel, showPlanLevelLabels, planStop, #F23645)
        target1LevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(target1LevelLabel, showPlanLevelLabels, planTarget1, #22C55E)
        target2LevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(target2LevelLabel, showPlanLevelLabels and planTargetCount >= 2, planTarget2, #22C55E)
        target3LevelLabel := f_replace_plan_level_label(target3LevelLabel, showPlanLevelLabels and planTargetCount >= 3, planTarget3, #22C55E)
    else
        planDirection := 0
        planActive := false
        planStartBar := na
        planEntry := na
        planStop := na
        planTarget1 := na
        planTarget2 := na
        planTarget3 := na
        planTargetCount := 3
        planHighSinceEntry := na
        planLowSinceEntry := na
        planScore := 0
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := false
        planHigherRisk := false
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        target3HitBar := na

// عزل تحديث الرسومات في دالة يقلل حجم النطاق الرئيسي. الدالة لا تعيد
// إسناد مراجع عامة؛ هي تعدل خصائص line/label فقط، وهي أنواع مرجعية.
f_update_plan_level_label(label id, int x, float level, float offset, string caption, color textColor) =>
    label.set_xy(id, x, level + offset)
    label.set_text(id, caption + f_price(level))
    label.set_color(id, color.new(color.black, 100))
    label.set_textcolor(id, textColor)

f_refresh_plan_display() =>
    if not na(entryLevelLabel)
        int x = bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float offset = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 3.0, safeAtr * 0.04)
        string entryCaption = planDirection == 1 ? (planHigherRisk ? "شراء فني - مخاطرة أعلى " : "دخول شراء ") : (planHigherRisk ? "بيع فني - مخاطرة أعلى " : "دخول بيع ")
        f_update_plan_level_label(entryLevelLabel, x, planEntry, offset, entryCaption, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor)
        f_update_plan_level_label(stopLevelLabel, x, planStop, offset, "وقف ", #F23645)
        f_update_plan_level_label(target1LevelLabel, x, planTarget1, offset, "هدف 1" + (target1Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        if planTargetCount >= 2
            f_update_plan_level_label(target2LevelLabel, x, planTarget2, offset, "هدف 2" + (target2Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
        if planTargetCount >= 3
            f_update_plan_level_label(target3LevelLabel, x, planTarget3, offset, "هدف 3" + (target3Done ? " ✓ " : " "), #22C55E)
    true
f_refresh_plan_display()

// =============================================================================
// Arabic display and theory dashboard
// =============================================================================
// شمعة الدخول المؤكدة لها الأولوية البصرية حتى لو كان تلوين الاتجاه العام
// معطلا. بقية الشموع لا تتغير إلا إذا فعّل المستخدم تلوين الاتجاه.
barcolor(stockChartAllowed and newPlanCreatedEvent ? (planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor) : na)
// إشارات الشارت بقالب موحد؛ الاختلاف في النص واللون والاتجاه فقط.
int maximumUnifiedChartLabels = beginnerDisplayMode ? 10 : (18)
bool unifiedEventMarkerNow = chartSignalDisplayAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and unifiedEventMarkerEnabled and not newPlanCreatedEvent
if unifiedEventMarkerNow
    f_push_chart_marker(chartEventLabels, maximumUnifiedChartLabels, unifiedEventBar, unifiedEventLevel, unifiedEventText, unifiedEventDirection >= 0, unifiedEventColor, unifiedEventAtr, 0.84)

if stockChartAllowed
    if newPlanCreatedEvent
        string entryMarkerText = planDirection == 1 ? (planHigherRisk ? "شراء فني" : "شراء") : (planHigherRisk ? "بيع فني" : "بيع")
        activePlanEntryMarker := f_replace_chart_marker(activePlanEntryMarker, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? low : high, entryMarkerText, planDirection == 1, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, safeAtr, 1.38)
    if planActive
        if target1Done and target1HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget1Marker := f_replace_chart_marker(activePlanTarget1Marker, bar_index, planTarget1, "هدف 1", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.44)
        if target2Done and target2HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget2Marker := f_replace_chart_marker(activePlanTarget2Marker, bar_index, planTarget2, "هدف 2", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.44)
        if target3Done and target3HitBar == bar_index
            activePlanTarget3Marker := f_replace_chart_marker(activePlanTarget3Marker, bar_index, planTarget3, "هدف 3", planDirection == -1, #22C55E, safeAtr, 0.44)
    if planEndedThisBar and not na(planOutcomePrice)
        bool outcomeBelow = planOutcomeKind == 3 ? planDirection == -1 : planDirection == 1
        string outcomeText = planOutcomeKind == 1 ? "وقف" : planOutcomeKind == 3 ? (planTargetCount == 1 ? "هدف 1" : "هدف 3") : "نتيجة ملتبسة"
        color outcomeColor = planOutcomeKind == 3 ? #22C55E : planOutcomeKind == 1 ? #F23645 : #F59E0B
        activePlanOutcomeMarker := f_replace_chart_marker(activePlanOutcomeMarker, bar_index, planOutcomePrice, outcomeText, outcomeBelow, outcomeColor, safeAtr, 1.66)

// تنبيهات بشرية مختصرة؛ جميعها تعتمد على شمعة مؤكدة أو حدث إدارة مسجل.
bool longPlanAlert = stockChartAllowed and newPlanCreatedEvent and planDirection == 1
bool shortPlanAlert = stockChartAllowed and newPlanCreatedEvent and planDirection == -1
bool protectiveStopAlert = stockChartAllowed and planOutcomeBar == bar_index and planOutcomeKind == 1
bool confirmedFakeoutAlert = stockChartAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout)
bool target1Alert = stockChartAllowed and target1HitBar == bar_index
bool target2Alert = stockChartAllowed and target2HitBar == bar_index
bool target3Alert = stockChartAllowed and target3HitBar == bar_index
bool ambiguousPlanAlert = stockChartAllowed and planOutcomeBar == bar_index and planOutcomeKind == 5

// جسر Webhook الديناميكي. يسمح بإرسال عدة أهداف بالترتيب عند قفزة سعرية واحدة.
f_send_bot_alerts() =>
    if enableBotWebhookAlerts and stockChartAllowed and barstate.isconfirmed
        string alertSource = planHigherRisk ? "SC-V94.6-D-HR" : "SC-V94.6-D"
        if longPlanAlert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "NL", planEntry, 1, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        else if shortPlanAlert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "NS", planEntry, -1, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        if target1Alert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "T1", planTarget1, planDirection, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        if target2Alert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "T2", planTarget2, planDirection, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        if target3Alert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "T3", planTarget3, planDirection, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        if protectiveStopAlert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "SL", planOutcomePrice, planDirection, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        else if ambiguousPlanAlert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "AMB", planOutcomePrice, planDirection, planEntry, planStop, planTarget1, planTarget2, planTarget3, planTargetCount, planScore, planScoreMax, planCounterTrend), alert.freq_all)
        else if confirmedFakeoutAlert and not newPlanCreatedEvent and not target1Alert and not target2Alert and not target3Alert
            alert(f_bot_payload(alertSource, "FO", close, 0, na, na, na, na, na, 1, 0, 1, false), alert.freq_all)
    true

f_send_bot_alerts()
